# CMR ERP System — Complete End-to-End Blueprint

## Explicit Assumptions (proceeding with defaults)

text

```
✅ CMR = Commerce & Retail Management
✅ Industry = Retail / Distribution (multi-branch)
✅ Branches = 5 locations, 1 country (India)
✅ Concurrent Users = ~300 users
✅ Daily Transactions = ~8,000 (orders, invoices, stock movements)
✅ Online-only (stable internet at all branches)
✅ Compliance = GST, e-invoicing (IRN/QR), TDS, Labor laws
✅ Integrations = Payment gateway, SMS, Email, Bank reconciliation
✅ Infrastructure = Cloud (AWS/Azure), PostgreSQL RDS
✅ Uptime SLA = 99.5%
✅ Multi-tenant = No (single org, multi-branch)
✅ Security = PII heavy (customer data, employee data, bank details)
```

---

# PHASE 1 — ERP Modules + Automation

---

## 1.1 Complete Module Map

text

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                        CMR ERP SYSTEM                           │
├──────────────┬──────────────┬──────────────┬────────────────────┤
│   CORE       │  OPERATIONS  │   PEOPLE     │   INTELLIGENCE     │
├──────────────┼──────────────┼──────────────┼────────────────────┤
│ Identity &   │ Sales        │ HR &         │ Reporting &        │
│ Access       │ Management   │ Payroll      │ Analytics          │
│              │              │              │                    │
│ Finance &    │ Purchase     │ CRM          │ Audit & Compliance │

│              │              │ Asset        │ Notifications      │
│ Branch &     │ Inventory    │ Management   │ & Alerts           │
│ Config       │ Management   │              │                    │
└──────────────┴──────────────┴──────────────┴────────────────────┘

## 1.2 Module Definitions

---

### MODULE 1: Identity & Access Management (IAM)

text

```
PURPOSE:
Controls who can log in and what they can see or do in the system.
Think of it as the "security guard" of the ERP.

KEY FEATURES:
├── User registration, login, logout
├── Role-Based Access Control (RBAC)
├── Permission-level granularity (view/create/edit/delete/approve)
├── Branch-level data isolation
├── Session management + JWT tokens
├── Multi-factor authentication (MFA)
├── Password policies + account lockout
└── Login audit trail

MAIN ENTITIES (nouns):
├── User
├── Role
├── Permission
├── Branch
└── LoginSession

MAIN WORKFLOWS (verbs):
├── Register user → Assign role → Assign branch
├── Login → Validate credentials → Issue JWT → Track session
├── Request access → Admin approve → Grant permission
└── Forgot password → OTP verify → Reset
```

---

### MODULE 2: Finance & Accounting

text

```
PURPOSE:
Manages all money coming in and going out. Keeps the company's
books clean and ready for tax filing.

KEY FEATURES:
├── Chart of Accounts (CoA)
├── General Ledger (GL)
├── Accounts Payable (AP) & Receivable (AR)
├── Journal Entries (manual + auto)
├── Bank Reconciliation
├── GST computation + e-invoicing (IRN)
├── TDS management
├── Financial statements (P&L, Balance Sheet, Cash Flow)
├── Multi-period closing
└── Budget management

MAIN ENTITIES (nouns):
├── Account (ChartOfAccount)
├── JournalEntry + JournalLine
├── Invoice (Sales + Purchase)
├── Payment
├── BankAccount + BankTransaction
├── TaxRate + GSTReturn
├── Budget
└── FinancialPeriod

MAIN WORKFLOWS (verbs):
├── Raise invoice → Post to GL → Track receivable → Receive payment
├── Receive bill → Post to GL → Track payable → Make payment
├── Reconcile bank → Match transactions → Identify gaps
├── Close period → Lock entries → Generate reports
└── Generate GST returns → File e-invoice → Track IRN
```

---

### MODULE 3: Sales Management

text

```
PURPOSE:
Handles everything from taking a customer order to delivering
goods and collecting money.

KEY FEATURES:
├── Customer quotation
├── Sales order management
├── Delivery/shipment tracking
├── Sales invoice (with GST)
├── Returns & credit notes
├── Price lists + discounts + schemes
├── Sales targets vs actuals
└── Branch-wise sales performance

MAIN ENTITIES (nouns):
├── Customer
├── Quotation + QuotationItem
├── SalesOrder + SalesOrderItem
├── Delivery + DeliveryItem
├── SalesInvoice + SalesInvoiceItem
├── CreditNote
├── PriceList + PricingRule
└── SalesTarget

MAIN WORKFLOWS (verbs):
├── Create quotation → Customer accepts → Convert to sales order
├── Sales order → Pick & pack → Create delivery → Ship
├── Delivery confirmed → Generate invoice → Collect payment
├── Customer returns goods → Create credit note → Adjust AR
└── Set sales target → Track actuals → Report variance
```

---

### MODULE 4: Purchase Management

text

```
PURPOSE:
Handles buying goods and services from suppliers. Ensures
we never overpay and always have what we need.

KEY FEATURES:
├── Purchase Requisition (PR)
├── Request for Quotation (RFQ) from suppliers
├── Purchase Order (PO)
├── Goods Receipt Note (GRN)
├── Supplier invoice matching (3-way match)
├── Purchase returns
├── Supplier evaluation
└── Approval workflows

MAIN ENTITIES (nouns):
├── Supplier
├── PurchaseRequisition + PRItem
├── RequestForQuotation + RFQItem
├── PurchaseOrder + POItem
├── GoodsReceiptNote + GRNItem
├── SupplierInvoice
├── PurchaseReturn
└── SupplierEvaluation

MAIN WORKFLOWS (verbs):
├── Create PR → Manager approves → Send RFQ to suppliers
├── Compare supplier quotes → Select best → Create PO
├── Receive goods → Create GRN → 3-way match (PO+GRN+Invoice)
├── Pay supplier → Post payment → Close payable
└── Return damaged goods → Debit note → Credit from supplier
```

---

### MODULE 5: Inventory Management

text

```
PURPOSE:
Tracks every product in every warehouse/branch. Knows
exactly how much stock we have, where it is, and when
to reorder.

KEY FEATURES:
├── Multi-warehouse/branch stock
├── Product catalog (SKU, barcode, HSN code)
├── Stock receipts + issues
├── Inter-branch transfers
├── Stock adjustments + write-offs
├── Batch/lot/serial number tracking
├── Reorder point + auto-replenishment alerts
├── Stock valuation (FIFO/Weighted Average)
└── Physical stock count / cycle counts

MAIN ENTITIES (nouns):
├── Product + ProductVariant
├── Category + Brand
├── Warehouse + WarehouseLocation (bin/shelf)
├── StockEntry (receipt/issue/transfer/adjustment)
├── StockLedger
├── Batch + SerialNumber
├── ReorderRule
└── StockCount + StockCountItem

MAIN WORKFLOWS (verbs):
├── Add product → Set reorder levels → Monitor stock
├── Receive stock (GRN) → Update ledger → Update valuation
├── Sales dispatch → Reduce stock → Post cost of goods sold
├── Transfer stock between branches → Confirm receipt
├── Run stock count → Compare with system → Post adjustments
└── Stock falls below reorder → Trigger PR automatically
```

---

PURPOSE:

KEY FEATURES:

MAIN ENTITIES (nouns):

├── Opportunity + OpportunityStage
├── Contact + Account (customer company)
├── Activity (call/meeting/email/note)
├── Complaint + ComplaintResolution
└── LoyaltyAccount + LoyaltyTransaction

MAIN WORKFLOWS (verbs):
├── Capture lead → Qualify → Convert to opportunity
├── Opportunity → Propose → Negotiate → Win/Lose
├── Log customer call → Schedule follow-up → Close activity
├── Customer complaint → Assign agent → Resolve → Rate
└── Earn loyalty points on purchase → Redeem on next purchase

├── Apply leave → Manager approves → Deduct from balance
├── Month-end → Auto-compute payroll → Approve → Disburse

## 1.3 Automation Design

---

### AUTOMATION 1: Auto Stock Reorder

text

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ELI10 (Like you're 10 years old):                           │
│                                                             │
│ Imagine you have a jar of cookies. You set a rule that says │
│ "when fewer than 5 cookies are left, automatically order    │
│ 20 more cookies from the cookie factory."                   │
│                                                             │
│ The ERP watches the cookie jar all day. The moment it sees  │
│ less than 5 cookies, it automatically sends an order to the │
│ factory WITHOUT anyone having to do anything!               │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

TECHNICAL EXPLANATION:
├── Trigger: StockEntry posted → StockLedger updated
├── Event: StockUpdatedDomainEvent raised
├── Handler: ReorderCheckHandler subscribes to event
├── Logic: 
│     SELECT current_qty FROM stock_ledger WHERE product_id = X
│     Compare with reorder_point from ReorderRule table
│     IF current_qty <= reorder_point THEN
├── Action: Create PurchaseRequisition automatically
│           Set status = 'System Generated'
│           Notify Purchase Manager via email + in-app
├── Background Job: Hangfire RecurringJob (daily 2AM)
│     Additional safety sweep for missed real-time events
└── Idempotency: Check if open PR already exists for product
```

---

### AUTOMATION 2: Invoice Auto-Generation

text

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ELI10:                                                      │
│                                                             │
│ When you order a pizza and the delivery guy brings it, the  │
│ restaurant automatically prints your bill. You don't have   │
│ to ask them to make a bill - it just happens when your      │
│ pizza is delivered!                                         │
│                                                             │
│ The ERP does the same. When goods are delivered and         │
│ confirmed, the invoice is created automatically with all    │
│ the right amounts and taxes calculated.                     │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

TECHNICAL EXPLANATION:
├── Trigger: Delivery status changed to 'Confirmed'
├── Event: DeliveryConfirmedDomainEvent
├── Handler: InvoiceGenerationHandler
├── Logic:
│     Fetch SalesOrder linked to Delivery
│     Calculate line-item amounts + GST (CGST/SGST/IGST)
│     Generate IRN (e-invoice) via GST portal API
│     Create SalesInvoice record with IRN + QR code
├── Action: 
│     Post journal entry to GL (Dr AR, Cr Revenue, Cr Tax)
│     Send invoice PDF to customer via email/WhatsApp
│     Update AR aging report
└── GST API Integration: NIC e-invoice sandbox/production API
```

---

### AUTOMATION 3: Approval Workflows

text

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ELI10:                                                      │
│                                                             │
│ Imagine before your mom buys something expensive, she asks  │
│ your dad. If it's VERY expensive, she asks your grandpa     │
│ too. The more expensive it is, the more people need to say  │
│ "yes" before buying.                                        │
│                                                             │
│ The ERP has the same rule. Small purchases are approved by  │
│ a manager. Big purchases need the director to say yes too.  │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

TECHNICAL EXPLANATION:
├── Approval Matrix Table (configurable rules):
│     Amount Range    → Approver Role
│     0–10,000       → Branch Manager
│     10,001–1,00,000 → Regional Manager
│     >1,00,000       → Finance Director + CEO
│
├── Workflow Engine:
│     ApprovalWorkflow entity with steps
│     State machine: Draft→Pending→Approved/Rejected
│     Each approval step: assigned to role, deadline, escalation
│
├── Trigger: Document submitted (PR, Expense, Leave)
├── Event: ApprovalRequestedEvent
├── Handler: WorkflowOrchestrationService
│     Creates ApprovalStep records
│     Sends notification to approver
│
├── Escalation: Hangfire job checks overdue steps
│     If not approved within SLA hours → escalate to next level
│
└── Audit: Every approve/reject logged with timestamp + comment
```

---

### AUTOMATION 4: Payroll Auto-Scheduling

text

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ELI10:                                                      │
│                                                             │
│ Every month on the same day, your parents get their salary  │
│ in the bank. Nobody has to tell the company "hey it's       │
│ salary day!" - it just happens automatically because it's   │
│ scheduled.                                                  │
│                                                             │
│ The ERP knows: every 28th of the month, calculate everyone's│
│ pay, deduct taxes, and send money to their accounts.        │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

TECHNICAL EXPLANATION:
├── Hangfire RecurringJob: Cron = "0 2 28 * *" (2AM on 28th)
├── Steps:
│     1. Fetch all active employees for pay period
│     2. Compute gross: basic + HRA + allowances
│     3. Compute deductions: PF(12%), ESI(0.75%), PT, TDS
│     4. Net = Gross - Deductions
│     5. Generate PaySlip records
│     6. Create bank payment file (NEFT batch)
│     7. Post journal entries (Dr Salary Expense, Cr Bank)
│     8. Email payslips to employees
├── Approval: Payroll report sent to HR Manager for approval
│     After approval → Disburse + mark as paid
└── TDS: Monthly TDS challan auto-generated
```

---

### AUTOMATION 5: Bank Reconciliation Auto-Matching

text

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ELI10:                                                      │
│                                                             │
│ Imagine your piggy bank has money going in and out. You     │
│ have a notebook where you wrote every time you added or     │
│ took out money. Reconciliation is when you check: does my   │
│ notebook match what's actually in the piggy bank?           │
│                                                             │
│ The ERP does this automatically! It reads your bank         │
│ statement and matches each line with your records.          │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

TECHNICAL EXPLANATION:
├── Integration: Bank statement upload (CSV/XLSX/OFX format)
│               OR bank API (account aggregator in India)
├── Matching Algorithm:
│     For each BankTransaction:
│       - Match by Amount + Date (±2 days) + Reference
│       - Confidence score: 100% exact, 85% amount+date
│       - Auto-reconcile if confidence ≥ 95%
│       - Flag for manual review if < 95%
├── Background Job: Hangfire (daily at 6AM)
│     Download bank statement
│     Run matching engine
│     Update BankTransaction.ReconcileStatus
├── Unmatched Transactions:
│     Alert Finance team via in-app notification
│     Aging dashboard for unreconciled items
└── Output: Reconciliation report PDF + lock reconciled period
```

## 1.4 Cross-Cutting Features

text

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ CROSS-CUTTING FEATURES                                       │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                              │
│ 1. AUDIT LOG                                                 │
│    Every create/update/delete logged with:                   │
│    - Who (UserId), When (timestamp), What (entity+field)     │
│    - Old value → New value                                   │
│    - IP address + device                                     │
│    - Tamper-proof (append-only table)                        │
│                                                              │
│ 2. NOTIFICATIONS (Multi-channel)                             │
│    - Email: SendGrid / AWS SES                               │
│    - SMS: Twilio / MSG91                                     │
│    - In-App: SignalR real-time push                          │
│    - WhatsApp Business API (invoices, alerts)                │
│                                                              │
│ 3. DOCUMENT MANAGEMENT                                       │
│    - Attach files to any entity (invoice, PO, employee)      │
│    - Stored in AWS S3 / Azure Blob                           │
│    - Virus scan on upload                                    │
│    - Signed URLs for secure access                           │
│                                                              │
│ 4. LOCALIZATION                                              │
│    - Currency: INR (primary), USD (reporting optional)       │
│    - Timezone: IST (UTC+5:30)                                │
│    - Date format: DD/MM/YYYY                                 │
│    - Language: English (extendable to Hindi/regional)        │
│    - Number format: Indian (lakhs, crores)                   │
│                                                              │
│ 5. BRANCH ISOLATION                                          │
│    - Every transaction tagged with BranchId                  │
│    - Row-level filtering by branch                           │
│    - Head office sees ALL branches                           │
│    - Branch user sees ONLY own branch                        │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

# PHASE 2 — Database Schema (PostgreSQL)

---

## 2.1 High-Level ERD (Text)

text

```
[users] ──── [user_profiles] ──── [branches]
   │
   ├── [user_roles] ──── [roles] ──── [role_permissions] ──── [permissions]
   │
[tenants] (future)

[customers] ──── [contacts]
    │
    ├── [quotations] ──── [quotation_items] ──── [products]
    │         │
    │    [sales_orders] ──── [sales_order_items]
    │         │
    │    [deliveries] ──── [delivery_items]
    │         │
    │    [sales_invoices] ──── [sales_invoice_items]
    │         │
    │    [payments_received]
    │
[suppliers] ──── [purchase_requisitions] ──── [pr_items]
    │                    │
    │           [purchase_orders] ──── [po_items]
    │                    │
    │           [goods_receipt_notes] ──── [grn_items]
    │                    │
    │           [supplier_invoices] ──── [supplier_invoice_items]
    │
[products] ──── [product_variants] ──── [product_categories]
    │
    ├── [stock_ledger] ──── [warehouses] ──── [warehouse_locations]
    ├── [stock_entries] ──── [stock_entry_items]
    └── [reorder_rules]

[chart_of_accounts] ──── [journal_entries] ──── [journal_lines]
[bank_accounts] ──── [bank_transactions]
[financial_periods]

[employees] ──── [departments] ──── [designations]
    │
    ├── [attendance_records]
    ├── [leave_requests] ──── [leave_types]
    ├── [payroll_runs] ──── [pay_slips] ──── [payslip_items]
    └── [expense_claims] ──── [expense_items]

[assets] ──── [asset_categories] ──── [depreciation_schedules]
[audit_logs]
[notifications]
[attachments]
[approval_workflows] ──── [approval_steps]
[outbox_messages]
```

---

## 2.2 Table-by-Table Schema

---

### SCHEMA: Identity & Access

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- BRANCHES
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE branches (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    code            VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name            VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    address         TEXT,
    city            VARCHAR(100),
    state           VARCHAR(100),
    pincode         VARCHAR(10),
    phone           VARCHAR(20),
    email           VARCHAR(200),
    gstin           VARCHAR(15),
    is_head_office  BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by      UUID,
    deleted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,          -- soft delete
    deleted_by      UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_branches_code 
    ON branches(code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

-- ============================================================
-- ASP.NET CORE IDENTITY TABLES (customized)
-- Framework generates: AspNetUsers, AspNetRoles, etc.
-- We extend with:
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE user_profiles (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    user_id         UUID NOT NULL,        -- FK to AspNetUsers.Id
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    employee_id     UUID,                 -- FK to employees (nullable)
    full_name       VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    display_name    VARCHAR(100),
    avatar_url      TEXT,
    phone           VARCHAR(20),
    preferred_lang  VARCHAR(10) DEFAULT 'en',
    timezone        VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'Asia/Kolkata',
    last_login_at   TIMESTAMPTZ,
    last_login_ip   INET,
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by      UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_user_profiles_user_id ON user_profiles(user_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_user_profiles_branch_id ON user_profiles(branch_id);

-- ============================================================
-- PERMISSIONS (fine-grained)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE permissions (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    module          VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,   -- e.g. 'Sales'
    action          VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,   -- e.g. 'Invoice.Create'
    description     TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_permissions_module_action 
    ON permissions(module, action);

-- ============================================================
-- ROLE_PERMISSIONS (many-to-many)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE role_permissions (
    role_id         UUID NOT NULL,          -- FK to AspNetRoles.Id
    permission_id   UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES permissions(id),
    granted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    granted_by      UUID,
    PRIMARY KEY (role_id, permission_id)
);
```

---

### SCHEMA: Finance & Accounting

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- FINANCIAL PERIODS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE financial_periods (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    name            VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,   -- e.g. 'Apr 2024'
    start_date      DATE NOT NULL,
    end_date        DATE NOT NULL,
    is_closed       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    closed_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    closed_by       UUID,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_financial_periods_dates 
    ON financial_periods(start_date, end_date);

-- ============================================================
-- CHART OF ACCOUNTS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE chart_of_accounts (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    account_code    VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    account_name    VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    account_type    VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL 
                    CHECK (account_type IN (
                        'Asset','Liability','Equity',
                        'Revenue','Expense'
                    )),
    account_subtype VARCHAR(50),
    parent_id       UUID REFERENCES chart_of_accounts(id),
    is_group        BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_system       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,  -- cannot delete
    normal_balance  VARCHAR(6) NOT NULL 
                    CHECK (normal_balance IN ('Debit','Credit')),
    currency        VARCHAR(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'INR',
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by      UUID,
    deleted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by      UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_coa_code 
    ON chart_of_accounts(account_code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_coa_parent_id ON chart_of_accounts(parent_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_coa_type ON chart_of_accounts(account_type);

-- ============================================================
-- JOURNAL ENTRIES
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE journal_entries (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    entry_number    VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    entry_date      DATE NOT NULL,
    period_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES financial_periods(id),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    entry_type      VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL
                    CHECK (entry_type IN (
                        'Manual','SalesInvoice','PurchaseInvoice',
                        'Payment','Receipt','Payroll','Depreciation',
                        'Adjustment','Opening'
                    )),
    reference_type  VARCHAR(50),            -- e.g. 'SalesInvoice'
    reference_id    UUID,                   -- FK to source document
    narration       TEXT NOT NULL,
    total_debit     NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL,
    total_credit    NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL,
    is_posted       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    posted_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    posted_by       UUID,
    is_reversed     BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    reversal_of_id  UUID REFERENCES journal_entries(id),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by      UUID,
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_je_balanced 
        CHECK (total_debit = total_credit)
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_je_number ON journal_entries(entry_number);
CREATE INDEX ix_je_date ON journal_entries(entry_date DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_je_branch_date ON journal_entries(branch_id, entry_date);
CREATE INDEX ix_je_reference ON journal_entries(reference_type, reference_id);

-- ============================================================
-- JOURNAL LINES
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE journal_lines (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    journal_entry_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES journal_entries(id),
    account_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES chart_of_accounts(id),
    debit_amount    NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    credit_amount   NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    description     TEXT,
    cost_center_id  UUID,                   -- future use
    line_order      SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_jl_one_side 
        CHECK (
            (debit_amount > 0 AND credit_amount = 0) OR
            (credit_amount > 0 AND debit_amount = 0)
        )
);

CREATE INDEX ix_jl_journal_entry ON journal_lines(journal_entry_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_jl_account ON journal_lines(account_id);

-- ============================================================
-- BANK ACCOUNTS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE bank_accounts (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    account_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES chart_of_accounts(id),
    bank_name       VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    account_number  VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,   -- encrypted
    ifsc_code       VARCHAR(11),
    account_type    VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL
                    CHECK (account_type IN ('Current','Savings','OD')),
    currency        VARCHAR(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'INR',
    opening_balance NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_bank_account_number 
    ON bank_accounts(account_number, bank_name);

-- ============================================================
-- BANK TRANSACTIONS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE bank_transactions (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    bank_account_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES bank_accounts(id),
    transaction_date    DATE NOT NULL,
    value_date          DATE,
    description         TEXT NOT NULL,
    reference_number    VARCHAR(100),
    debit_amount        NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    credit_amount       NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    balance             NUMERIC(18,2),
    reconcile_status    VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Unreconciled'
                        CHECK (reconcile_status IN (
                            'Unreconciled','Matched','Reconciled','Flagged'
                        )),
    reconciled_with_id  UUID,               -- journal_lines.id
    reconciled_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    reconciled_by       UUID,
    confidence_score    NUMERIC(5,2),       -- matching score 0-100
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_bt_bank_account ON bank_transactions(bank_account_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_bt_date ON bank_transactions(transaction_date);
CREATE INDEX ix_bt_reconcile_status 
    ON bank_transactions(reconcile_status) 
    WHERE reconcile_status = 'Unreconciled';
```

---

### SCHEMA: Products & Inventory

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- PRODUCT CATEGORIES
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE product_categories (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    code            VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    parent_id       UUID REFERENCES product_categories(id),
    description     TEXT,
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    deleted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by      UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_product_categories_code 
    ON product_categories(code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

-- ============================================================
-- PRODUCTS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE products (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    sku             VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    barcode         VARCHAR(50),
    name            VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    description     TEXT,
    category_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES product_categories(id),
    brand           VARCHAR(100),
    hsn_code        VARCHAR(10),            -- GST HSN/SAC code
    unit_of_measure VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,   -- PCS, KG, LTR, etc.
    product_type    VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL
                    CHECK (product_type IN (
                        'Product','Service','Bundle'
                    )),
    is_serialized   BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_batched      BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    -- Pricing
    standard_cost   NUMERIC(18,4),
    list_price      NUMERIC(18,4),
    -- Tax
    gst_rate        NUMERIC(5,2),           -- 5, 12, 18, 28
    -- Dimensions (for shipping)
    weight_kg       NUMERIC(10,4),
    length_cm       NUMERIC(10,2),
    width_cm        NUMERIC(10,2),
    height_cm       NUMERIC(10,2),
    -- Images
    image_url       TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by      UUID,
    deleted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by      UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_products_sku 
    ON products(sku) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_products_barcode 
    ON products(barcode) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND barcode IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_products_category ON products(category_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_products_name ON products USING gin(to_tsvector('english', name));

-- ============================================================
-- WAREHOUSES
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE warehouses (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    code            VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    address         TEXT,
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    deleted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_warehouses_code 
    ON warehouses(branch_id, code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

-- ============================================================
-- STOCK LEDGER (real-time stock by product+warehouse)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE stock_ledger (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    product_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES products(id),
    warehouse_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES warehouses(id),
    quantity_on_hand    NUMERIC(18,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    quantity_reserved   NUMERIC(18,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,  -- against SO
    quantity_available  NUMERIC(18,4) GENERATED ALWAYS AS 
                            (quantity_on_hand - quantity_reserved) STORED,
    weighted_avg_cost   NUMERIC(18,4),
    last_movement_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    CONSTRAINT uix_stock_ledger_product_warehouse 
        UNIQUE(product_id, warehouse_id),
    CONSTRAINT chk_qty_non_negative 
        CHECK (quantity_on_hand >= 0)
);

CREATE INDEX ix_stock_ledger_product ON stock_ledger(product_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_stock_ledger_warehouse ON stock_ledger(warehouse_id);

-- ============================================================
-- STOCK ENTRIES (movement journal)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE stock_entries (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    entry_number    VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    entry_type      VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL
                    CHECK (entry_type IN (
                        'GoodsReceipt','GoodsIssue','Transfer',
                        'Adjustment','StockCount','Return'
                    )),
    entry_date      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    from_warehouse_id UUID REFERENCES warehouses(id),
    to_warehouse_id   UUID REFERENCES warehouses(id),
    reference_type  VARCHAR(50),
    reference_id    UUID,
    notes           TEXT,
    status          VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Draft'
                    CHECK (status IN ('Draft','Posted','Cancelled')),
    posted_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    posted_by       UUID,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by      UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_stock_entries_number ON stock_entries(entry_number);
CREATE INDEX ix_stock_entries_date ON stock_entries(entry_date DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_stock_entries_branch ON stock_entries(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_stock_entries_reference 
    ON stock_entries(reference_type, reference_id);

-- ============================================================
-- STOCK ENTRY ITEMS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE stock_entry_items (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    stock_entry_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES stock_entries(id),
    product_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES products(id),
    quantity            NUMERIC(18,4) NOT NULL,
    unit_cost           NUMERIC(18,4),
    batch_number        VARCHAR(50),
    serial_number       VARCHAR(100),
    expiry_date         DATE,
    notes               TEXT,
    line_order          SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_sei_qty_positive CHECK (quantity > 0)
);

CREATE INDEX ix_sei_stock_entry ON stock_entry_items(stock_entry_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_sei_product ON stock_entry_items(product_id);

-- ============================================================
-- REORDER RULES
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE reorder_rules (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    product_id          UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES products(id),
    warehouse_id        UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES warehouses(id),
    reorder_point       NUMERIC(18,4) NOT NULL,
    reorder_qty         NUMERIC(18,4) NOT NULL,
    max_qty             NUMERIC(18,4),
    preferred_supplier_id UUID,
    is_active           BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by          UUID,
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    CONSTRAINT uix_reorder_product_warehouse 
        UNIQUE(product_id, warehouse_id)
);
```

### SCHEMA: Sales

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- CUSTOMERS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE customers (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    code            VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name            VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    customer_type   VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Retail'
                    CHECK (customer_type IN ('Retail','Wholesale','Corporate')),
    gstin           VARCHAR(15),
    pan             VARCHAR(10),            -- encrypted
    credit_limit    NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    credit_days     SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    payment_terms   VARCHAR(50),
    -- Contact
    email           VARCHAR(200),
    phone           VARCHAR(20),           -- encrypted
    -- Address
    billing_address TEXT,
    billing_city    VARCHAR(100),
    billing_state   VARCHAR(100),
    billing_pincode VARCHAR(10),
    -- State code for GST (place of supply)
    state_code      VARCHAR(2),
    -- Loyalty
    loyalty_points  NUMERIC(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    -- Meta
    branch_id       UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    assigned_to     UUID,                  -- sales rep user_id
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by      UUID,
    deleted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by      UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_customers_code 
    ON customers(code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_customers_name 
    ON customers USING gin(to_tsvector('english', name));
CREATE INDEX ix_customers_phone ON customers(phone);
CREATE INDEX ix_customers_gstin ON customers(gstin);

-- ============================================================
-- SALES ORDERS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE sales_orders (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    order_number    VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    order_date      DATE NOT NULL,
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    customer_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES customers(id),
    warehouse_id    UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES warehouses(id),
    salesperson_id  UUID,
    -- Pricing
    subtotal        NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    discount_amount NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    taxable_amount  NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    cgst_amount     NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    sgst_amount     NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    igst_amount     NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    total_amount    NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    -- Terms
    payment_terms   VARCHAR(50),
    delivery_date   DATE,
    notes           TEXT,
    -- Status
    status          VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Draft'
                    CHECK (status IN (
                        'Draft','Confirmed','Processing',
                        'Shipped','Delivered','Cancelled','Closed'
                    )),
    -- Audit
    confirmed_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    confirmed_by    UUID,
    cancelled_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    cancelled_by    UUID,
    cancellation_reason TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by      UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_so_number ON sales_orders(order_number);
CREATE INDEX ix_so_customer ON sales_orders(customer_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_so_branch_date ON sales_orders(branch_id, order_date DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_so_status ON sales_orders(status) 
    WHERE status NOT IN ('Delivered','Cancelled','Closed');

-- ============================================================
-- SALES ORDER ITEMS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE sales_order_items (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    sales_order_id  UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES sales_orders(id),
    product_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES products(id),
    quantity        NUMERIC(18,4) NOT NULL,
    unit_price      NUMERIC(18,4) NOT NULL,
    discount_pct    NUMERIC(5,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    discount_amount NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    taxable_amount  NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    gst_rate        NUMERIC(5,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    cgst_rate       NUMERIC(5,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    sgst_rate       NUMERIC(5,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    igst_rate       NUMERIC(5,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    cgst_amount     NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    sgst_amount     NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    igst_amount     NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    line_total      NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    qty_delivered   NUMERIC(18,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    qty_invoiced    NUMERIC(18,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    line_order      SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    notes           TEXT,
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_soi_qty_positive CHECK (quantity > 0)
);

CREATE INDEX ix_soi_order ON sales_order_items(sales_order_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_soi_product ON sales_order_items(product_id);

-- ============================================================
-- SALES INVOICES
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE sales_invoices (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    invoice_number      VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    invoice_date        DATE NOT NULL,
    due_date            DATE NOT NULL,
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    customer_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES customers(id),
    sales_order_id      UUID REFERENCES sales_orders(id),
    -- GST e-invoice
    irn                 VARCHAR(64),       -- Invoice Reference Number
    ack_number          VARCHAR(20),
    ack_date            TIMESTAMPTZ,
    qr_code             TEXT,              -- base64 QR
    e_invoice_status    VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'Pending'
                        CHECK (e_invoice_status IN (
                            'Pending','Generated','Failed','Cancelled'
                        )),
    -- Amounts
    subtotal            NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    discount_amount     NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    taxable_amount      NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    cgst_amount         NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    sgst_amount         NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    igst_amount         NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    total_amount        NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    amount_paid         NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    amount_outstanding  NUMERIC(18,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS 
                            (total_amount - amount_paid) STORED,
    -- Status
    payment_status      VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Unpaid'
                        CHECK (payment_status IN (
                            'Unpaid','PartiallyPaid','Paid','Overdue'
                        )),
    status              VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Draft'
                        CHECK (status IN (
                            'Draft','Posted','Cancelled'
                        )),
    -- Journal
    journal_entry_id    UUID REFERENCES journal_entries(id),
    -- Audit
    posted_at           TIMESTAMPTZ,
    posted_by           UUID,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by          UUID,
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by          UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_si_number ON sales_invoices(invoice_number);
CREATE INDEX ix_si_customer ON sales_invoices(customer_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_si_branch_date ON sales_invoices(branch_id, invoice_date DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_si_payment_status 
    ON sales_invoices(payment_status) 
    WHERE payment_status IN ('Unpaid','PartiallyPaid','Overdue');
CREATE INDEX ix_si_due_date ON sales_invoices(due_date);
```

---

### SCHEMA: HR & Payroll

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- DEPARTMENTS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE departments (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    code            VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    parent_id       UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    manager_id      UUID,                  -- employee_id
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    deleted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_departments_code ON departments(code) 
    WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

-- ============================================================
-- EMPLOYEES
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE employees (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    employee_code       VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    first_name          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    last_name           VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    date_of_birth       DATE,
    gender              VARCHAR(10),
    -- Employment
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    department_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES departments(id),
    designation         VARCHAR(100),
    employment_type     VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'FullTime'
                        CHECK (employment_type IN (
                            'FullTime','PartTime','Contract','Intern'
                        )),
    date_of_joining     DATE NOT NULL,
    date_of_leaving     DATE,
    status              VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Active'
                        CHECK (status IN (
                            'Active','Resigned','Terminated','OnLeave'
                        )),
    -- Salary
    ctc_annual          NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    basic_salary        NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    hra                 NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    -- Contact (sensitive)
    personal_email      VARCHAR(200),      -- encrypted
    work_email          VARCHAR(200),
    phone               VARCHAR(20),       -- encrypted
    -- IDs (sensitive)
    pan_number          VARCHAR(10),       -- encrypted
    aadhar_last4        VARCHAR(4),        -- only last 4 digits
    pf_number           VARCHAR(20),
    esi_number          VARCHAR(20),
    bank_account_number VARCHAR(50),       -- encrypted
    bank_ifsc           VARCHAR(11),
    -- Emergency
    emergency_contact_name  VARCHAR(200),
    emergency_contact_phone VARCHAR(20),   -- encrypted
    -- Audit
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by          UUID,
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by          UUID,
    deleted_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by          UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_employees_code 
    ON employees(employee_code) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_employees_branch ON employees(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_employees_department ON employees(department_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_employees_status ON employees(status) WHERE status = 'Active';

-- ============================================================
-- ATTENDANCE RECORDS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE attendance_records (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    employee_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES employees(id),
    attendance_date     DATE NOT NULL,
    check_in            TIMESTAMPTZ,
    check_out           TIMESTAMPTZ,
    work_hours          NUMERIC(5,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
                            CASE 
                                WHEN check_in IS NOT NULL 
                                AND check_out IS NOT NULL
                                THEN ROUND(
                                    EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (check_out - check_in)) 
                                    / 3600, 2
                                )
                                ELSE 0 
                            END
                        ) STORED,
    status              VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Present'
                        CHECK (status IN (
                            'Present','Absent','HalfDay','Holiday',
                            'Leave','WeekOff'
                        )),
    source              VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Manual'
                        CHECK (source IN ('Biometric','Manual','System')),
    remarks             TEXT,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by          UUID,
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by          UUID,
    
    CONSTRAINT uix_attendance_employee_date 
        UNIQUE(employee_id, attendance_date)
);

CREATE INDEX ix_attendance_employee ON attendance_records(employee_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_attendance_date ON attendance_records(attendance_date);

-- ============================================================
-- PAYROLL RUNS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE payroll_runs (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    run_number      VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    pay_period_from DATE NOT NULL,
    pay_period_to   DATE NOT NULL,
    pay_date        DATE NOT NULL,
    branch_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    total_employees INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    total_gross     NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    total_deductions NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    total_net       NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    status          VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Draft'
                    CHECK (status IN (
                        'Draft','Processing','PendingApproval',
                        'Approved','Disbursed','Cancelled'
                    )),
    approved_at     TIMESTAMPTZ,
    approved_by     UUID,
    disbursed_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    disbursed_by    UUID,
    journal_entry_id UUID REFERENCES journal_entries(id),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by      UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_payroll_run_number ON payroll_runs(run_number);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_payroll_period_branch 
    ON payroll_runs(pay_period_from, pay_period_to, branch_id)
    WHERE status NOT IN ('Cancelled');

-- ============================================================
-- PAY SLIPS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE pay_slips (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    payroll_run_id  UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payroll_runs(id),
    employee_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES employees(id),
    -- Earnings
    basic           NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    hra             NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    other_allowance NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    gross_salary    NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    -- Deductions
    pf_employee     NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    esi_employee    NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    professional_tax NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    tds             NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    other_deductions NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    total_deductions NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    -- Net
    net_salary      NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    -- Attendance summary
    working_days    SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    present_days    NUMERIC(5,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    absent_days     NUMERIC(5,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    leave_days      NUMERIC(5,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    -- Payment
    payment_mode    VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Bank'
                    CHECK (payment_mode IN ('Bank','Cash','Cheque')),
    payment_status  VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Pending'
                    CHECK (payment_status IN (
                        'Pending','Paid','Failed','OnHold'
                    )),
    paid_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    CONSTRAINT uix_payslip_run_employee 
        UNIQUE(payroll_run_id, employee_id)
);

CREATE INDEX ix_payslip_payroll_run ON pay_slips(payroll_run_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_payslip_employee ON pay_slips(employee_id);
```

---

### SCHEMA: Cross-Cutting

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- AUDIT LOGS (append-only, tamper-proof)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE audit_logs (
    id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,  -- sequential for ordering
    entity_type     VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    entity_id       UUID NOT NULL,
    action          VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL
                    CHECK (action IN (
                        'Create','Update','Delete','Login',
                        'Logout','Approve','Reject','View'
                    )),
    changes         JSONB,                  -- {field: {old: x, new: y}}
    user_id         UUID,
    user_name       VARCHAR(200),
    branch_id       UUID,
    ip_address      INET,
    user_agent      TEXT,
    timestamp       TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    request_id      UUID                    -- correlation ID
);

-- Partitioned by month for performance
CREATE INDEX ix_audit_entity ON audit_logs(entity_type, entity_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_audit_user ON audit_logs(user_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_audit_timestamp ON audit_logs(timestamp DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_audit_action ON audit_logs(action);

-- ============================================================
-- NOTIFICATIONS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE notifications (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    user_id         UUID NOT NULL,
    title           VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    body            TEXT NOT NULL,
    type            VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL
                    CHECK (type IN (
                        'Info','Warning','Success','Error','Approval'
                    )),
    module          VARCHAR(50),
    reference_type  VARCHAR(50),
    reference_id    UUID,
    channel         VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'InApp'
                    CHECK (channel IN ('InApp','Email','SMS','WhatsApp')),
    is_read         BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    read_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    sent_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    send_status     VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Pending'
                    CHECK (send_status IN (
                        'Pending','Sent','Failed','Skipped'
                    )),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_notifications_user_unread 
    ON notifications(user_id, is_read) 
    WHERE is_read = FALSE;
CREATE INDEX ix_notifications_created ON notifications(created_at DESC);

-- ============================================================
-- ATTACHMENTS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE attachments (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    entity_type     VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    entity_id       UUID NOT NULL,
    file_name       VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    original_name   VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    content_type    VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    file_size_bytes BIGINT NOT NULL,
    storage_path    TEXT NOT NULL,         -- S3/Azure Blob path
    storage_bucket  VARCHAR(100),
    is_public       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    uploaded_by     UUID NOT NULL,
    uploaded_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    deleted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by      UUID
);

CREATE INDEX ix_attachments_entity 
    ON attachments(entity_type, entity_id) 
    WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;

-- ============================================================
-- OUTBOX MESSAGES (Transactional Outbox Pattern)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE outbox_messages (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    message_type    VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    payload         JSONB NOT NULL,
    status          VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Pending'
                    CHECK (status IN ('Pending','Processing','Sent','Failed')),
    retry_count     SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    max_retries     SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 3,
    last_error      TEXT,
    scheduled_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    processed_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_outbox_status_scheduled 
    ON outbox_messages(status, scheduled_at) 
    WHERE status IN ('Pending','Failed');

-- ============================================================
-- APPROVAL WORKFLOWS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE approval_workflows (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    entity_type     VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    entity_id       UUID NOT NULL,
    current_step    SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    total_steps     SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    status          VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Pending'
                    CHECK (status IN (
                        'Pending','Approved','Rejected','Cancelled'
                    )),
    submitted_by    UUID NOT NULL,
    submitted_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    completed_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    notes           TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_approval_entity 
    ON approval_workflows(entity_type, entity_id)
    WHERE status = 'Pending';

CREATE TABLE approval_steps (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    workflow_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES approval_workflows(id),
    step_number         SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    approver_role       VARCHAR(100),
    approver_user_id    UUID,
    status              VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Pending'
                        CHECK (status IN (
                            'Pending','Approved','Rejected',
                            'Escalated','Skipped'
                        )),
    due_at              TIMESTAMPTZ,
    decided_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    decided_by          UUID,
    comments            TEXT,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_approval_steps_workflow ON approval_steps(workflow_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_approval_steps_approver 
    ON approval_steps(approver_user_id, status) 
    WHERE status = 'Pending';
```

# PHASE 3 — API Design

---

## 3.1 Auth Strategy

text

```
CHOICE: JWT Bearer Tokens (not cookies)
REASON: 
  - Mobile/API clients need stateless tokens
  - SPA frontends work better with Bearer auth
  - Easier cross-origin (CORS) handling
  - Refresh tokens stored in HttpOnly cookie (best of both)

FLOW:
  POST /api/v1/auth/login
    → Validate credentials via Identity
    → Issue Access Token (15 min expiry, JWT)
    → Issue Refresh Token (7 days, stored in HttpOnly cookie)
    → Return access token in response body

  POST /api/v1/auth/refresh
    → Read refresh token from HttpOnly cookie
    → Validate → Issue new access token

  POST /api/v1/auth/logout
    → Revoke refresh token
    → Clear cookie
```

---

## 3.2 Global API Conventions

text

```
BASE URL:    https://api.cmrerp.com/api/v1
VERSIONING:  URL path versioning: /api/v1/, /api/v2/
PAGINATION:  ?page=1&pageSize=20&sortBy=createdAt&sortDir=desc
FILTERING:   ?status=Active&branchId=xxx&search=john
CONTENT:     application/json
AUTH:        Authorization: Bearer {token}
IDEMPOTENCY: Idempotency-Key: {uuid} header for POST/PUT payments

STANDARD ERROR FORMAT:
{
  "type": "https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc7807",
  "title": "Validation Error",
  "status": 422,
  "detail": "One or more validation errors occurred",
  "instance": "/api/v1/sales/orders",
  "traceId": "00-abc123-def456-00",
  "errors": {
    "CustomerId": ["Customer not found"],
    "Items": ["At least one item is required"]
  }
}

STANDARD SUCCESS FORMAT:
{
  "success": true,
  "data": { ... },
  "meta": {
    "page": 1,
    "pageSize": 20,
    "totalCount": 150,
    "totalPages": 8
  }
}
```

## 3.4 Sales Module Endpoints

http

```
# CUSTOMERS
GET    /api/v1/customers                    # List with pagination + filters
POST   /api/v1/customers                    # Create customer
GET    /api/v1/customers/{id}               # Get by id
PUT    /api/v1/customers/{id}               # Update customer
DELETE /api/v1/customers/{id}               # Soft delete
GET    /api/v1/customers/{id}/ledger        # Customer outstanding
GET    /api/v1/customers/{id}/orders        # Customer orders history

# SALES ORDERS
GET    /api/v1/sales/orders                 # List
POST   /api/v1/sales/orders                 # Create draft
GET    /api/v1/sales/orders/{id}            # Get detail
PUT    /api/v1/sales/orders/{id}            # Update draft
POST   /api/v1/sales/orders/{id}/confirm    # Confirm order
POST   /api/v1/sales/orders/{id}/cancel     # Cancel order
GET    /api/v1/sales/orders/{id}/deliveries # Linked deliveries

# SALES INVOICES
GET    /api/v1/sales/invoices               # List
POST   /api/v1/sales/invoices               # Create manually
GET    /api/v1/sales/invoices/{id}          # Get detail
POST   /api/v1/sales/invoices/{id}/post     # Post to GL
POST   /api/v1/sales/invoices/{id}/cancel   # Cancel invoice
GET    /api/v1/sales/invoices/{id}/pdf      # Download PDF
POST   /api/v1/sales/invoices/{id}/send-email # Email to customer

# PAYMENTS RECEIVED
POST   /api/v1/sales/payments               # Record payment
GET    /api/v1/sales/payments/{id}          # Get payment
```

csharp

```
// REQUEST DTOs
public record CreateSalesOrderRequest(
    Guid CustomerId,
    Guid WarehouseId,
    DateOnly DeliveryDate,
    string? PaymentTerms,
    string? Notes,
    List<SalesOrderItemRequest> Items
);

public record SalesOrderItemRequest(
    Guid ProductId,
    decimal Quantity,
    decimal UnitPrice,
    decimal DiscountPct
);

// RESPONSE DTOs
public record SalesOrderResponse(
    Guid Id,
    string OrderNumber,
    DateOnly OrderDate,
    string CustomerName,
    string Status,
    decimal SubTotal,
    decimal DiscountAmount,
    decimal TaxableAmount,
    decimal CgstAmount,
    decimal SgstAmount,
    decimal IgstAmount,
    decimal TotalAmount,
    List<SalesOrderItemResponse> Items,
    DateTime CreatedAt
);

// VALIDATION RULES:
// - CustomerId: required, must exist, must be active
// - WarehouseId: required, must belong to user's branch
// - Items: min 1 item, max 100 items
// - Quantity: > 0, max 99999
// - UnitPrice: > 0
// - DiscountPct: 0-100
// - DeliveryDate: >= today
```

## 3.8 OpenAPI/Swagger Structure

YAML

```
openapi: "3.1.0"
info:
  title: "CMR ERP API"
  version: "1.0.0"
  description: "Commerce & Retail Management ERP"
  
servers:
  - url: "https://api.cmrerp.com/api/v1"
    description: "Production"
  - url: "https://sandbox-api.cmrerp.com/api/v1"
    description: "Sandbox"

security:
  - BearerAuth: []

components:
  securitySchemes:
    BearerAuth:
      type: http
      scheme: bearer
      bearerFormat: JWT
      
  schemas:
    ProblemDetails:    # RFC 7807
    PagedResponse:     # Generic paged wrapper
    SalesOrderRequest: # ...
    SalesOrderResponse: # ...
    # (all DTOs here)
    
tags:
  - name: Auth
  - name: Sales
  - name: Purchase
  - name: Inventory
  - name: Finance
  - name: HR
  - name: CRM
  - name: Assets
  - name: Reports
```

## 4.2 Dependency Rules

text

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│              DEPENDENCY FLOW (strict)                  │
│                                                        │
│  Api.Layer ──────────→ Application.Layer               │
│      │                       │                         │
│      │                       ↓                         │
│      │              Domain.Layer (pure)                 │
│      │                       ↑                         │
│      │                       │                         │
│      └──────→ Infrastructure.Layer                     │
│                              │                         │
│                              └→ Domain.Layer           │
│                                                        │
│  RULES:                                                │
│  ✅ Domain has ZERO external dependencies              │
│  ✅ Application depends on Domain only                 │
│  ✅ Infrastructure implements Application interfaces   │
│  ✅ Api depends on Application (sends commands/queries)│
│  ❌ Domain NEVER references Application or Infra       │
│  ❌ Modules NEVER reference other module's internals   │
│  ✅ Modules communicate via BuildingBlocks.Contracts   │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

## 4.3 Code Examples

### Example 1: Aggregate Root

csharp

```
// CMR.Modules.Sales.Domain/Aggregates/SalesOrder/SalesOrder.cs

public sealed class SalesOrder : AggregateRoot<Guid>
{
    private readonly List<SalesOrderItem> _items = new();
    
    public string OrderNumber { get; private set; }
    public DateOnly OrderDate { get; private set; }
    public Guid BranchId { get; private set; }
    public Guid CustomerId { get; private set; }
    public Guid WarehouseId { get; private set; }
    public SalesOrderStatus Status { get; private set; }
    public Money SubTotal { get; private set; }
    public Money DiscountAmount { get; private set; }
    public Money TaxableAmount { get; private set; }
    public Money TotalTax { get; private set; }
    public Money TotalAmount { get; private set; }
    public string? Notes { get; private set; }
    public IReadOnlyList<SalesOrderItem> Items => _items.AsReadOnly();

    private SalesOrder() { } // EF Core

    public static SalesOrder Create(
        string orderNumber,
        Guid branchId,
        Guid customerId,
        Guid warehouseId,
        string? notes)
    {
        var order = new SalesOrder
        {
            Id = Guid.NewGuid(),
            OrderNumber = orderNumber,
            OrderDate = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.UtcNow),
            BranchId = branchId,
            CustomerId = customerId,
            WarehouseId = warehouseId,
            Status = SalesOrderStatus.Draft,
            Notes = notes,
            SubTotal = Money.Zero,
            DiscountAmount = Money.Zero,
            TaxableAmount = Money.Zero,
            TotalTax = Money.Zero,
            TotalAmount = Money.Zero
        };

        order.RaiseDomainEvent(new SalesOrderCreatedEvent(order.Id));
        return order;
    }

    public void AddItem(
        Guid productId,
        decimal quantity,
        decimal unitPrice,
        decimal discountPct,
        decimal gstRate)
    {
        if (Status != SalesOrderStatus.Draft)
            throw new DomainException("Cannot modify a confirmed order.");

        if (quantity <= 0)
            throw new DomainException("Quantity must be positive.");

        var existingItem = _items.FirstOrDefault(i => i.ProductId == productId);
        if (existingItem is not null)
        {
            existingItem.UpdateQuantity(existingItem.Quantity + quantity);
        }
        else
        {
            var item = SalesOrderItem.Create(
                Id, productId, quantity, unitPrice, discountPct, gstRate);
            _items.Add(item);
        }

        RecalculateTotals();
    }

    public void Confirm()
    {
        if (Status != SalesOrderStatus.Draft)
            throw new DomainException("Only draft orders can be confirmed.");

        if (!_items.Any())
            throw new DomainException("Order must have at least one item.");

        Status = SalesOrderStatus.Confirmed;
        RaiseDomainEvent(new SalesOrderConfirmedEvent(Id, CustomerId, TotalAmount));
    }

    public void Cancel(string reason)
    {
        if (Status is SalesOrderStatus.Delivered or SalesOrderStatus.Closed)
            throw new DomainException("Cannot cancel delivered or closed orders.");

        Status = SalesOrderStatus.Cancelled;
        RaiseDomainEvent(new SalesOrderCancelledEvent(Id, reason));
    }

    private void RecalculateTotals()
    {
        var subTotal = _items.Sum(i => i.Quantity * i.UnitPrice);
        var discount = _items.Sum(i => i.DiscountAmount.Amount);
        var taxable = subTotal - discount;
        var tax = _items.Sum(i => i.TotalTax.Amount);

        SubTotal = Money.From(subTotal);
        DiscountAmount = Money.From(discount);
        TaxableAmount = Money.From(taxable);
        TotalTax = Money.From(tax);
        TotalAmount = Money.From(taxable + tax);
    }
}

// EF Core Configuration
public class SalesOrderConfiguration : IEntityTypeConfiguration<SalesOrder>
{
    public void Configure(EntityTypeBuilder<SalesOrder> builder)
    {
        builder.ToTable("sales_orders");
        builder.HasKey(x => x.Id);

        builder.Property(x => x.OrderNumber)
            .HasMaxLength(30)
            .IsRequired();

        builder.Property(x => x.Status)
            .HasConversion<string>()
            .HasMaxLength(30)
            .IsRequired();

        builder.OwnsOne(x => x.SubTotal, m =>
        {
            m.Property(p => p.Amount)
                .HasColumnName("subtotal")
                .HasColumnType("numeric(18,2)");
            m.Property(p => p.Currency)
                .HasColumnName("currency")
                .HasMaxLength(3);
        });

        // Similar for other Money properties...

        builder.HasMany(x => x.Items)
            .WithOne()
            .HasForeignKey("sales_order_id")
            .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

        builder.HasIndex(x => x.OrderNumber)
            .IsUnique();

        // Soft delete query filter
        builder.HasQueryFilter(x => x.DeletedAt == null);
    }
}
```

### Example 2: Command Handler

csharp

```
// CMR.Modules.Sales.Application/Orders/Commands/CreateSalesOrder/
// CreateSalesOrderHandler.cs

public sealed class CreateSalesOrderCommand : ICommand<SalesOrderResponse>
{
    public Guid CustomerId { get; init; }
    public Guid WarehouseId { get; init; }
    public DateOnly DeliveryDate { get; init; }
    public string? Notes { get; init; }
    public List<OrderItemDto> Items { get; init; } = new();
}

public sealed class CreateSalesOrderHandler
    : ICommandHandler<CreateSalesOrderCommand, SalesOrderResponse>
{
    private readonly ISalesOrderRepository _orderRepository;
    private readonly ICustomerRepository _customerRepository;
    private readonly IProductQueryService _productService;   // cross-module contract
    private readonly IOrderNumberGenerator _numberGenerator;
    private readonly ICurrentUserService _currentUser;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;

    public CreateSalesOrderHandler(
        ISalesOrderRepository orderRepository,
        ICustomerRepository customerRepository,
        IProductQueryService productService,
        IOrderNumberGenerator numberGenerator,
        ICurrentUserService currentUser,
        IUnitOfWork unitOfWork)
    {
        _orderRepository = orderRepository;
        _customerRepository = customerRepository;
        _productService = productService;
        _numberGenerator = numberGenerator;
        _currentUser = currentUser;
        _unitOfWork = unitOfWork;
    }

    public async Task<SalesOrderResponse> Handle(
        CreateSalesOrderCommand command,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        // 1. Validate customer exists
        var customer = await _customerRepository.GetByIdAsync(
            command.CustomerId, cancellationToken)
            ?? throw new NotFoundException(
                nameof(Customer), command.CustomerId);

        // 2. Generate unique order number
        var orderNumber = await _numberGenerator
            .GenerateAsync("SO", _currentUser.BranchId, cancellationToken);

        // 3. Create aggregate
        var order = SalesOrder.Create(
            orderNumber,
            _currentUser.BranchId,
            command.CustomerId,
            command.WarehouseId,
            command.Notes);

        // 4. Add items with product validation + GST
        foreach (var item in command.Items)
        {
            var product = await _productService.GetProductAsync(
                item.ProductId, cancellationToken)
                ?? throw new NotFoundException(
                    nameof(Product), item.ProductId);

            order.AddItem(
                item.ProductId,
                item.Quantity,
                item.UnitPrice,
                item.DiscountPct,
                product.GstRate);
        }

        // 5. Persist + save domain events
        await _orderRepository.AddAsync(order, cancellationToken);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);

        // 6. Map to response
        return SalesOrderResponse.FromDomain(order);
    }
}

// Validator
public sealed class CreateSalesOrderValidator
    : AbstractValidator<CreateSalesOrderCommand>
{
    public CreateSalesOrderValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.CustomerId)
            .NotEmpty()
            .WithMessage("Customer is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.WarehouseId)
            .NotEmpty()
            .WithMessage("Warehouse is required.");

        RuleFor(x => x.Items)
            .NotEmpty()
            .WithMessage("At least one item is required.")
            .Must(items => items.Count <= 100)
            .WithMessage("Maximum 100 items per order.");

        RuleForEach(x => x.Items).ChildRules(item =>
        {
            item.RuleFor(i => i.ProductId)
                .NotEmpty();

            item.RuleFor(i => i.Quantity)
                .GreaterThan(0)
                .LessThanOrEqualTo(99999);

            item.RuleFor(i => i.UnitPrice)
                .GreaterThan(0);

            item.RuleFor(i => i.DiscountPct)
                .InclusiveBetween(0, 100);
        });
    }
}
```

### Example 3: Dapper Query Handler

csharp

```
// CMR.Modules.Sales.Application/Orders/Queries/GetSalesOrders/
// GetSalesOrdersHandler.cs

public sealed record GetSalesOrdersQuery : IQuery<PagedResult<SalesOrderListItem>>
{
    public int Page { get; init; } = 1;
    public int PageSize { get; init; } = 20;
    public string? Search { get; init; }
    public string? Status { get; init; }
    public Guid? CustomerId { get; init; }
    public DateOnly? FromDate { get; init; }
    public DateOnly? ToDate { get; init; }
    public string SortBy { get; init; } = "order_date";
    public string SortDir { get; init; } = "desc";
}

public sealed class GetSalesOrdersHandler
    : IQueryHandler<GetSalesOrdersQuery, PagedResult<SalesOrderListItem>>
{
    private readonly IDbConnectionFactory _connectionFactory;
    private readonly ICurrentUserService _currentUser;

    public GetSalesOrdersHandler(
        IDbConnectionFactory connectionFactory,
        ICurrentUserService currentUser)
    {
        _connectionFactory = connectionFactory;
        _currentUser = currentUser;
    }

    public async Task<PagedResult<SalesOrderListItem>> Handle(
        GetSalesOrdersQuery query,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        // Dapper: raw SQL for maximum performance on reads
        const string sql = """
            WITH filtered AS (
                SELECT
                    so.id,
                    so.order_number,
                    so.order_date,
                    c.name         AS customer_name,
                    c.code         AS customer_code,
                    c.phone        AS customer_phone,
                    so.total_amount,
                    so.status,
                    so.delivery_date,
                    up.full_name   AS created_by_name,
                    so.created_at,
                    COUNT(soi.id)  AS item_count,
                    COUNT(*)       OVER() AS total_count
                FROM sales_orders so
                INNER JOIN customers c ON c.id = so.customer_id
                INNER JOIN user_profiles up ON up.user_id = so.created_by
                LEFT JOIN sales_order_items soi ON soi.sales_order_id = so.id
                WHERE
                    so.branch_id = @BranchId
                    AND so.deleted_at IS NULL
                    AND (@Status IS NULL OR so.status = @Status)
                    AND (@CustomerId IS NULL OR so.customer_id = @CustomerId)
                    AND (@FromDate IS NULL OR so.order_date >= @FromDate)
                    AND (@ToDate IS NULL OR so.order_date <= @ToDate)
                    AND (
                        @Search IS NULL
                        OR so.order_number ILIKE @SearchPattern
                        OR c.name ILIKE @SearchPattern
                        OR c.code ILIKE @SearchPattern
                    )
                GROUP BY
                    so.id, so.order_number, so.order_date,
                    c.name, c.code, c.phone,
                    so.total_amount, so.status, so.delivery_date,
                    up.full_name, so.created_at
            )
            SELECT *
            FROM filtered
            ORDER BY
                CASE WHEN @SortBy = 'order_date' AND @SortDir = 'asc'
                    THEN order_date END ASC,
                CASE WHEN @SortBy = 'order_date' AND @SortDir = 'desc'
                    THEN order_date END DESC,
                CASE WHEN @SortBy = 'order_number' AND @SortDir = 'asc'
                    THEN order_number END ASC,
                CASE WHEN @SortBy = 'total_amount' AND @SortDir = 'desc'
                    THEN total_amount END DESC
            LIMIT @PageSize OFFSET @Offset;
        """;

        using var connection = _connectionFactory.CreateConnection();

        var parameters = new
        {
            BranchId = _currentUser.BranchId,
            query.Status,
            query.CustomerId,
            FromDate = query.FromDate?.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
            ToDate = query.ToDate?.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MaxValue),
            Search = query.Search,
            SearchPattern = string.IsNullOrEmpty(query.Search)
                ? null
                : $"%{query.Search}%",
            SortBy = query.SortBy,
            SortDir = query.SortDir,
            PageSize = query.PageSize,
            Offset = (query.Page - 1) * query.PageSize
        };

        var rows = await connection.QueryAsync<SalesOrderListItem, long, (SalesOrderListItem, long)>(
            sql,
            (item, totalCount) => (item, totalCount),
            parameters,
            splitOn: "total_count");

        var resultList = rows.ToList();
        var totalCount = resultList.FirstOrDefault().Item2;

        return PagedResult<SalesOrderListItem>.Create(
            resultList.Select(r => r.Item1).ToList(),
            totalCount,
            query.Page,
            query.PageSize);
    }
}
```
