# PHASE 2 — Complete CRM Database Schema

---

## 2.1 ERD Overview

text

```
[contacts] ──── [contact_accounts] ──── [accounts]
     │
     ├── [leads] ──── [lead_scores] ──── [lead_activities]
     │         └──── [lead_tags] ──── [tags]
     │
     ├── [opportunities] ──── [opportunity_products]
     │           │ ──── [opportunity_competitors]
     │           └──── [opportunity_activities]
     │
     ├── [activities] (calls/emails/meetings/tasks)
     │
     ├── [tickets] ──── [ticket_comments]
     │       │ ──── [ticket_escalations]
     │       └──── [sla_timers]
     │
     ├── [loyalty_accounts] ──── [loyalty_transactions]
     │           └──── [loyalty_tiers]
     │
     ├── [campaigns] ──── [campaign_recipients]
     │       │ ──── [campaign_metrics]
     │       └──── [drip_campaign_enrollments]
     │
     ├── [segments] ──── [segment_members]
     │       └──── [segment_rules]
     │
     ├── [rfm_scores]
     │
     └── [surveys] ──── [survey_questions]
               └──── [survey_responses] ──── [survey_answers]
```

---

## 2.2 Full Table Schema

SQL

```
-- ============================================================
-- CRM SCHEMA (all tables in crm schema for isolation)
-- ============================================================
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS crm;

-- ============================================================
-- ACCOUNTS (companies/organizations)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.accounts (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    account_number      VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name                VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    account_type        VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Prospect'
                        CHECK (account_type IN (
                            'Prospect','Customer','Partner',
                            'Competitor','Other'
                        )),
    industry            VARCHAR(100),
    annual_revenue      NUMERIC(18,2),
    employee_count      INT,
    website             VARCHAR(300),
    description         TEXT,
    -- GST / Tax
    gstin               VARCHAR(15),
    pan                 VARCHAR(10),
    -- Address
    billing_address     TEXT,
    billing_city        VARCHAR(100),
    billing_state       VARCHAR(100),
    billing_pincode     VARCHAR(10),
    billing_state_code  VARCHAR(2),
    shipping_address    TEXT,
    shipping_city       VARCHAR(100),
    shipping_state      VARCHAR(100),
    shipping_pincode    VARCHAR(10),
    -- Contact
    primary_phone       VARCHAR(20),
    primary_email       VARCHAR(200),
    -- Ownership
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    assigned_to         UUID,               -- user_id of account manager
    -- Score
    account_score       SMALLINT DEFAULT 0, -- 0-100
    -- Meta
    is_active           BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    tags                TEXT[],             -- PostgreSQL array
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by          UUID,
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by          UUID,
    deleted_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by          UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_accounts_number 
    ON crm.accounts(account_number) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_accounts_name 
    ON crm.accounts USING gin(to_tsvector('english', name));
CREATE INDEX ix_accounts_gstin 
    ON crm.accounts(gstin) WHERE gstin IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_accounts_branch 
    ON crm.accounts(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_accounts_assigned 
    ON crm.accounts(assigned_to);
CREATE INDEX ix_accounts_tags 
    ON crm.accounts USING gin(tags);

-- ============================================================
-- CONTACTS (individual persons)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.contacts (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    contact_number      VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    -- Name
    salutation          VARCHAR(10),
    first_name          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    last_name           VARCHAR(100),
    full_name           VARCHAR(200) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
                            COALESCE(salutation || ' ', '') ||
                            first_name ||
                            COALESCE(' ' || last_name, '')
                        ) STORED,
    -- Role
    job_title           VARCHAR(100),
    department          VARCHAR(100),
    is_decision_maker   BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    -- Contact info (sensitive)
    email               VARCHAR(200),          -- encrypted
    phone               VARCHAR(20),           -- encrypted
    mobile              VARCHAR(20),           -- encrypted
    whatsapp_number     VARCHAR(20),
    -- Personal
    date_of_birth       DATE,
    gender              VARCHAR(10),
    -- Preferences
    preferred_language  VARCHAR(10) DEFAULT 'en',
    preferred_channel   VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'Email'
                        CHECK (preferred_channel IN (
                            'Email','SMS','WhatsApp','Phone'
                        )),
    -- Opt-in/out
    email_opt_in        BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    sms_opt_in          BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    whatsapp_opt_in     BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    opted_out_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    opted_out_reason    TEXT,
    -- Ownership
    branch_id           UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    assigned_to         UUID,
    -- Link to system customer
    customer_id         UUID,                 -- FK to customers table
    -- Meta
    source              VARCHAR(50),           -- where did this contact come from
    tags                TEXT[],
    is_active           BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by          UUID,
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by          UUID,
    deleted_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by          UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_contacts_number 
    ON crm.contacts(contact_number) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_contacts_email 
    ON crm.contacts(email) WHERE email IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_contacts_phone 
    ON crm.contacts(phone) WHERE phone IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_contacts_fullname 
    ON crm.contacts USING gin(to_tsvector('english', full_name));
CREATE INDEX ix_contacts_customer 
    ON crm.contacts(customer_id) WHERE customer_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_contacts_dob 
    ON crm.contacts(date_of_birth) WHERE date_of_birth IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_contacts_tags 
    ON crm.contacts USING gin(tags);

-- ============================================================
-- CONTACT_ACCOUNTS (many-to-many with role)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.contact_accounts (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    contact_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.contacts(id),
    account_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.accounts(id),
    role            VARCHAR(100),              -- e.g. 'CEO', 'Purchase Manager'
    is_primary      BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    
    CONSTRAINT uix_contact_account UNIQUE(contact_id, account_id)
);

CREATE INDEX ix_ca_contact ON crm.contact_accounts(contact_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_ca_account ON crm.contact_accounts(account_id);

-- ============================================================
-- LEAD SOURCES (lookup)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.lead_sources (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    channel         VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL
                    CHECK (channel IN (
                        'Website','Social','Referral','Event',
                        'Advertisement','WalkIn','SMS',
                        'WhatsApp','Email','Other'
                    )),
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_lead_sources_name ON crm.lead_sources(name);

-- ============================================================
-- LEADS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.leads (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    lead_number         VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    -- Basic info
    first_name          VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    last_name           VARCHAR(100),
    company_name        VARCHAR(300),
    job_title           VARCHAR(100),
    -- Contact (potentially unverified)
    email               VARCHAR(200),
    phone               VARCHAR(20),
    mobile              VARCHAR(20),
    -- Source
    source_id           UUID REFERENCES crm.lead_sources(id),
    utm_source          VARCHAR(100),
    utm_medium          VARCHAR(100),
    utm_campaign        VARCHAR(100),
    referrer_contact_id UUID REFERENCES crm.contacts(id),
    -- Qualification
    status              VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'New'
                        CHECK (status IN (
                            'New','Contacted','Qualified',
                            'Unqualified','Converted'
                        )),
    unqualified_reason  VARCHAR(200),
    -- Interest
    interest_product    VARCHAR(200),
    estimated_value     NUMERIC(18,2),
    expected_close_date DATE,
    -- Assignment
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    assigned_to         UUID,
    assigned_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    -- Scoring
    score               SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    score_grade         VARCHAR(1) 
                        GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
                            CASE
                                WHEN score >= 75 THEN 'A'
                                WHEN score >= 50 THEN 'B'
                                WHEN score >= 25 THEN 'C'
                                ELSE 'D'
                            END
                        ) STORED,
    -- Conversion
    converted_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    converted_to_opportunity_id UUID,
    converted_to_contact_id     UUID,
    -- Dedup
    is_duplicate        BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    duplicate_of_id     UUID REFERENCES crm.leads(id),
    -- Meta
    tags                TEXT[],
    notes               TEXT,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by          UUID,
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by          UUID,
    deleted_at          TIMESTAMPTZ
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_leads_number 
    ON crm.leads(lead_number) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_leads_status ON crm.leads(status);
CREATE INDEX ix_leads_branch ON crm.leads(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_leads_assigned ON crm.leads(assigned_to);
CREATE INDEX ix_leads_score ON crm.leads(score DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_leads_created ON crm.leads(created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_leads_email 
    ON crm.leads(email) WHERE email IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_leads_phone 
    ON crm.leads(phone) WHERE phone IS NOT NULL;

-- ============================================================
-- LEAD SCORES (detailed scoring breakdown)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.lead_scores (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    lead_id             UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.leads(id),
    profile_score       SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    behavior_score      SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    total_score         SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    score_breakdown     JSONB,           -- {signals: [{name, points}]}
    calculated_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    CONSTRAINT uix_lead_scores_lead UNIQUE(lead_id)
);

CREATE INDEX ix_lead_scores_total ON crm.lead_scores(total_score DESC);

-- ============================================================
-- LEAD ASSIGNMENT RULES
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.lead_assignment_rules (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    rule_name       VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    priority        SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    -- Conditions (JSON for flexibility)
    conditions      JSONB NOT NULL,     -- [{field, operator, value}]
    -- Assignment
    assign_to_user_id UUID,
    assign_to_role    VARCHAR(100),
    assign_to_branch  UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    -- Round robin within role
    use_round_robin BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ
);

-- ============================================================
-- OPPORTUNITY STAGES (configurable)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.opportunity_stages (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    name                VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    stage_order         SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    win_probability     NUMERIC(5,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
                        CHECK (win_probability BETWEEN 0 AND 100),
    is_won              BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_lost             BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_active           BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    color_hex           VARCHAR(7),
    stale_days          SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 7,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by          UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_opp_stages_order ON crm.opportunity_stages(stage_order);

-- ============================================================
-- OPPORTUNITIES
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.opportunities (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    opportunity_number  VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name                VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    -- Links
    contact_id          UUID REFERENCES crm.contacts(id),
    account_id          UUID REFERENCES crm.accounts(id),
    lead_id             UUID REFERENCES crm.leads(id),
    -- Stage
    stage_id            UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.opportunity_stages(id),
    win_probability     NUMERIC(5,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    -- Value
    estimated_value     NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    weighted_value      NUMERIC(18,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
                            ROUND(estimated_value * win_probability / 100, 2)
                        ) STORED,
    actual_value        NUMERIC(18,2),           -- populated on won
    currency            VARCHAR(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'INR',
    -- Dates
    expected_close_date DATE NOT NULL,
    actual_close_date   DATE,
    -- Outcome
    status              VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Open'
                        CHECK (status IN (
                            'Open','Won','Lost','Abandoned'
                        )),
    close_reason        TEXT,
    lost_to_competitor  VARCHAR(200),
    -- Ownership
    branch_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    assigned_to         UUID NOT NULL,
    -- Stale tracking
    last_activity_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    stale_alert_sent_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    is_stale            BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    -- Conversion
    converted_to_order_id UUID,             -- FK to sales_orders
    -- Meta
    description         TEXT,
    tags                TEXT[],
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by          UUID,
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by          UUID,
    deleted_at          TIMESTAMPTZ
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_opportunities_number 
    ON crm.opportunities(opportunity_number) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_opp_stage ON crm.opportunities(stage_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_opp_status ON crm.opportunities(status) 
    WHERE status = 'Open';
CREATE INDEX ix_opp_assigned ON crm.opportunities(assigned_to);
CREATE INDEX ix_opp_close_date ON crm.opportunities(expected_close_date);
CREATE INDEX ix_opp_stale 
    ON crm.opportunities(last_activity_at) 
    WHERE status = 'Open';
CREATE INDEX ix_opp_account ON crm.opportunities(account_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_opp_contact ON crm.opportunities(contact_id);

-- ============================================================
-- OPPORTUNITY PRODUCTS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.opportunity_products (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    opportunity_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.opportunities(id),
    product_id          UUID NOT NULL,          -- ref to products table
    product_name        VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,  -- snapshot at time of entry
    quantity            NUMERIC(18,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    unit_price          NUMERIC(18,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    discount_pct        NUMERIC(5,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    line_total          NUMERIC(18,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
                            ROUND(
                                quantity * unit_price 
                                * (1 - discount_pct / 100), 2
                            )
                        ) STORED,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_op_opportunity ON crm.opportunity_products(opportunity_id);

-- ============================================================
-- OPPORTUNITY COMPETITORS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.opportunity_competitors (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    opportunity_id  UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.opportunities(id),
    competitor_name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    strengths       TEXT,
    weaknesses      TEXT,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID
);

CREATE INDEX ix_oc_opportunity ON crm.opportunity_competitors(opportunity_id);

-- ============================================================
-- ACTIVITIES (unified activity log)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.activities (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    activity_type       VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL
                        CHECK (activity_type IN (
                            'Call','Email','Meeting','Demo',
                            'Visit','Note','Task','WhatsApp'
                        )),
    -- Polymorphic parent
    entity_type         VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL
                        CHECK (entity_type IN (
                            'Lead','Contact','Account','Opportunity','Ticket'
                        )),
    entity_id           UUID NOT NULL,
    -- Activity details
    subject             VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    description         TEXT,
    scheduled_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    started_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    ended_at            TIMESTAMPTZ,
    duration_minutes    INT,
    -- Outcome
    outcome             VARCHAR(50),
                        -- Call: Answered/Voicemail/NoAnswer/Busy
                        -- Email: Sent/Opened/Clicked/Bounced
                        -- Meeting: Held/Cancelled/Rescheduled
    outcome_notes       TEXT,
    next_action         TEXT,
    next_action_date    DATE,
    -- Status
    status              VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Scheduled'
                        CHECK (status IN (
                            'Scheduled','InProgress','Completed',
                            'Cancelled','Overdue'
                        )),
    -- Ownership
    assigned_to         UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id           UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    -- Email tracking
    email_message_id    VARCHAR(200),
    email_opened_at     TIMESTAMPTZ,
    email_clicked_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    -- Meta
    is_system_generated BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by          UUID,
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by          UUID
);

CREATE INDEX ix_activities_entity 
    ON crm.activities(entity_type, entity_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_activities_assigned_status 
    ON crm.activities(assigned_to, status) 
    WHERE status IN ('Scheduled','InProgress','Overdue');
CREATE INDEX ix_activities_scheduled 
    ON crm.activities(scheduled_at) 
    WHERE status = 'Scheduled';
CREATE INDEX ix_activities_type ON crm.activities(activity_type);

-- ============================================================
-- TAGS (reusable across leads, contacts, accounts)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.tags (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    color_hex       VARCHAR(7),
    module          VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'CRM',
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_tags_name ON crm.tags(name, module);

-- ============================================================
-- LOYALTY TIERS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.loyalty_tiers (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    name                VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,   -- Bronze, Silver, Gold, Platinum
    tier_order          SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    min_points          INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    max_points          INT,                    -- NULL = unlimited (top tier)
    earn_rate           NUMERIC(5,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1.0, 
                        -- points per spend unit
    spend_per_point     NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 100.00,
                        -- spend ₹100 = N points
    points_multiplier   NUMERIC(5,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1.0,
    perks               TEXT[],                 -- array of perk descriptions
    color_hex           VARCHAR(7),
    badge_icon          VARCHAR(100),
    is_active           BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by          UUID,
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_loyalty_tier_order ON crm.loyalty_tiers(tier_order);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_loyalty_tier_name ON crm.loyalty_tiers(name);

-- ============================================================
-- LOYALTY ACCOUNTS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.loyalty_accounts (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    account_number          VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    contact_id              UUID REFERENCES crm.contacts(id),
    customer_id             UUID,               -- FK to customers
    tier_id                 UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.loyalty_tiers(id),
    -- Balance
    points_balance          NUMERIC(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    points_lifetime_earned  NUMERIC(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    points_lifetime_redeemed NUMERIC(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    points_lifetime_expired NUMERIC(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    -- QR / Card
    loyalty_card_number     VARCHAR(20),
    qr_code_data            TEXT,
    -- Tier tracking
    tier_qualified_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    tier_review_date        DATE,               -- when to re-evaluate tier
    -- Referral
    referral_code           VARCHAR(20),
    referred_by_id          UUID REFERENCES crm.loyalty_accounts(id),
    -- Status
    is_active               BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at              TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by              UUID,
    updated_at              TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by              UUID,
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_la_balance_non_negative 
        CHECK (points_balance >= 0)
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_loyalty_account_number 
    ON crm.loyalty_accounts(account_number);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_loyalty_card_number 
    ON crm.loyalty_accounts(loyalty_card_number) 
    WHERE loyalty_card_number IS NOT NULL;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_loyalty_referral_code 
    ON crm.loyalty_accounts(referral_code) 
    WHERE referral_code IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_loyalty_customer 
    ON crm.loyalty_accounts(customer_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_loyalty_tier 
    ON crm.loyalty_accounts(tier_id);

-- ============================================================
-- LOYALTY RULES (earn rules - configurable)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.loyalty_rules (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    rule_name           VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    rule_type           VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL
                        CHECK (rule_type IN (
                            'Purchase','Referral','Birthday',
                            'Anniversary','Event','Manual','Bonus'
                        )),
    -- Conditions
    applicable_tier_ids UUID[],         -- NULL = all tiers
    applicable_product_category_ids UUID[], -- NULL = all products
    min_purchase_amount NUMERIC(18,2),
    -- Earning
    points_type         VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Fixed'
                        CHECK (points_type IN ('Fixed','PercentOfAmount','Multiplier')),
    points_value        NUMERIC(10,4) NOT NULL,
    max_points_per_transaction NUMERIC(10,2),
    -- Validity
    valid_from          DATE NOT NULL,
    valid_to            DATE,
    -- Status
    is_active           BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    priority            SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by          UUID,
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ
);

-- ============================================================
-- LOYALTY TRANSACTIONS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.loyalty_transactions (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    loyalty_account_id  UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.loyalty_accounts(id),
    transaction_type    VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL
                        CHECK (transaction_type IN (
                            'Earn','Redeem','Expire','Adjust',
                            'Refund','Bonus','Referral'
                        )),
    points              NUMERIC(12,2) NOT NULL, -- positive=earn, negative=debit
    balance_after       NUMERIC(12,2) NOT NULL,
    -- Source
    reference_type      VARCHAR(50),             -- 'SalesInvoice','Campaign'
    reference_id        UUID,
    rule_id             UUID REFERENCES crm.loyalty_rules(id),
    -- Expiry (for earned points)
    expires_at          DATE,
    is_expired          BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    -- Description
    description         TEXT NOT NULL,
    -- Meta
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by          UUID,
    is_reversed         BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    reversed_by_id      UUID REFERENCES crm.loyalty_transactions(id)
);

CREATE INDEX ix_lt_account ON crm.loyalty_transactions(loyalty_account_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_lt_type ON crm.loyalty_transactions(transaction_type);
CREATE INDEX ix_lt_expires 
    ON crm.loyalty_transactions(expires_at) 
    WHERE is_expired = FALSE AND expires_at IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_lt_reference 
    ON crm.loyalty_transactions(reference_type, reference_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_lt_created ON crm.loyalty_transactions(created_at DESC);

-- ============================================================
-- SEGMENTS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.segments (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    description     TEXT,
    segment_type    VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Dynamic'
                    CHECK (segment_type IN ('Static','Dynamic','RFM')),
    entity_type     VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Contact'
                    CHECK (entity_type IN ('Contact','Account','Lead')),
    -- For dynamic segments
    refresh_frequency VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'Daily'
                    CHECK (refresh_frequency IN (
                        'Realtime','Hourly','Daily','Weekly','Manual'
                    )),
    last_refreshed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    member_count    INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    -- Meta
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by      UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_segments_name ON crm.segments(name);

-- ============================================================
-- SEGMENT RULES
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.segment_rules (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    segment_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.segments(id),
    rule_group      SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1, -- groups are OR-ed
    field_name      VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    operator        VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL
                    CHECK (operator IN (
                        'equals','not_equals','contains','not_contains',
                        'greater_than','less_than','between',
                        'in','not_in','is_null','is_not_null'
                    )),
    field_value     TEXT,
    field_value2    TEXT,                        -- for 'between'
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_segment_rules_segment ON crm.segment_rules(segment_id);

-- ============================================================
-- SEGMENT MEMBERS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.segment_members (
    segment_id      UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.segments(id),
    entity_id       UUID NOT NULL,
    added_at        TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    PRIMARY KEY(segment_id, entity_id)
);

CREATE INDEX ix_segment_members_entity ON crm.segment_members(entity_id);

-- ============================================================
-- RFM SCORES
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.rfm_scores (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    customer_id         UUID NOT NULL,
    contact_id          UUID REFERENCES crm.contacts(id),
    -- Raw metrics
    last_purchase_date  DATE,
    purchase_count_12m  INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    total_spend_12m     NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    -- RFM scores (1-5)
    recency_score       SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1
                        CHECK (recency_score BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    frequency_score     SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1
                        CHECK (frequency_score BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    monetary_score      SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1
                        CHECK (monetary_score BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    rfm_total           SMALLINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
                            recency_score + frequency_score + monetary_score
                        ) STORED,
    -- Segment assignment
    rfm_segment         VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'New Customers',
    previous_segment    VARCHAR(50),
    segment_changed     BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    -- Calculated at
    calculated_at       TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    CONSTRAINT uix_rfm_customer UNIQUE(customer_id)
);

CREATE INDEX ix_rfm_segment ON crm.rfm_scores(rfm_segment);
CREATE INDEX ix_rfm_total ON crm.rfm_scores(rfm_total DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_rfm_calculated ON crm.rfm_scores(calculated_at DESC);

-- ============================================================
-- CAMPAIGNS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.campaigns (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    campaign_code       VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name                VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    description         TEXT,
    campaign_type       VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL
                        CHECK (campaign_type IN (
                            'Email','SMS','WhatsApp',
                            'Push','Multi','Drip'
                        )),
    -- Audience
    segment_id          UUID REFERENCES crm.segments(id),
    estimated_recipients INT DEFAULT 0,
    actual_recipients   INT DEFAULT 0,
    -- Content
    subject             VARCHAR(500),           -- for email
    template_id         UUID,                   -- FK to campaign_templates
    -- A/B Testing
    is_ab_test          BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    ab_test_split_pct   SMALLINT DEFAULT 50,    -- % to variant A
    ab_winner_variant   VARCHAR(1),             -- 'A' or 'B'
    ab_winner_selected_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    -- Schedule
    status              VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Draft'
                        CHECK (status IN (
                            'Draft','Scheduled','Sending',
                            'Sent','Paused','Cancelled','Failed'
                        )),
    scheduled_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    started_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    -- Budget
    budget_amount       NUMERIC(18,2),
    actual_cost         NUMERIC(18,2),
    -- UTM
    utm_source          VARCHAR(100),
    utm_medium          VARCHAR(100),
    -- Branch
    branch_id           UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    -- Meta
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by          UUID,
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by          UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_campaigns_code ON crm.campaigns(campaign_code);
CREATE INDEX ix_campaigns_status ON crm.campaigns(status);
CREATE INDEX ix_campaigns_scheduled ON crm.campaigns(scheduled_at) 
    WHERE status = 'Scheduled';

-- ============================================================
-- CAMPAIGN TEMPLATES
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.campaign_templates (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    name            VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    channel         VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    subject         VARCHAR(500),
    html_content    TEXT,
    text_content    TEXT,
    variables       JSONB,          -- [{name, description, default}]
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ
);

-- ============================================================
-- CAMPAIGN RECIPIENTS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.campaign_recipients (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    campaign_id     UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.campaigns(id),
    contact_id      UUID REFERENCES crm.contacts(id),
    email           VARCHAR(200),
    phone           VARCHAR(20),
    ab_variant      VARCHAR(1),                 -- 'A' or 'B'
    -- Delivery status
    send_status     VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Pending'
                    CHECK (send_status IN (
                        'Pending','Queued','Sent','Delivered',
                        'Failed','Bounced','Unsubscribed'
                    )),
    sent_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    delivered_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    error_message   TEXT,
    -- Engagement
    opened_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    clicked_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    click_count     SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    -- Conversion
    converted       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    converted_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    conversion_value NUMERIC(18,2),
    -- Meta
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_cr_campaign ON crm.campaign_recipients(campaign_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_cr_contact ON crm.campaign_recipients(contact_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_cr_status ON crm.campaign_recipients(send_status);
CREATE INDEX ix_cr_opened 
    ON crm.campaign_recipients(campaign_id, opened_at) 
    WHERE opened_at IS NOT NULL;

-- ============================================================
-- DRIP CAMPAIGNS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.drip_campaigns (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    name            VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    trigger_type    VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL
                    CHECK (trigger_type IN (
                        'CustomerCreated','LeadConverted',
                        'NoPurchase30Days','AfterPurchase',
                        'PostComplaintResolved','Birthday',
                        'ManualEnroll'
                    )),
    description     TEXT,
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ
);

-- ============================================================
-- DRIP CAMPAIGN STEPS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.drip_campaign_steps (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    drip_campaign_id    UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.drip_campaigns(id),
    step_number         SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    step_name           VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    channel             VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL
                        CHECK (channel IN (
                            'Email','SMS','WhatsApp','Task'
                        )),
    template_id         UUID REFERENCES crm.campaign_templates(id),
    -- Delay from previous step
    delay_hours         INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    -- Branching conditions
    send_condition      VARCHAR(50),   -- 'always','if_not_opened','if_clicked'
    exit_condition      VARCHAR(50),   -- 'if_purchased','if_unsubscribed'
    -- For Task step
    task_subject        VARCHAR(300),
    task_assign_to_role VARCHAR(100),
    
    CONSTRAINT uix_drip_step UNIQUE(drip_campaign_id, step_number)
);

CREATE INDEX ix_dcs_drip ON crm.drip_campaign_steps(drip_campaign_id);

-- ============================================================
-- DRIP CAMPAIGN ENROLLMENTS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.drip_campaign_enrollments (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    drip_campaign_id    UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.drip_campaigns(id),
    contact_id          UUID REFERENCES crm.contacts(id),
    customer_id         UUID,
    current_step_id     UUID REFERENCES crm.drip_campaign_steps(id),
    next_step_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    status              VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Active'
                        CHECK (status IN (
                            'Active','Completed','Converted',
                            'OptedOut','Paused'
                        )),
    enrolled_at         TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    completed_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    converted_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    exit_reason         VARCHAR(100),
    steps_completed     SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);

CREATE INDEX ix_dce_drip ON crm.drip_campaign_enrollments(drip_campaign_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_dce_next_step 
    ON crm.drip_campaign_enrollments(next_step_at, status) 
    WHERE status = 'Active';
CREATE INDEX ix_dce_contact ON crm.drip_campaign_enrollments(contact_id);

-- ============================================================
-- TICKET CATEGORIES
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.ticket_categories (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    name            VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    parent_id       UUID REFERENCES crm.ticket_categories(id),
    default_priority VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Medium'
                    CHECK (default_priority IN (
                        'Low','Medium','High','Critical'
                    )),
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_ticket_categories_name ON crm.ticket_categories(name);

-- ============================================================
-- SLA RULES
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.sla_rules (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    name                    VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    category_id             UUID REFERENCES crm.ticket_categories(id),
    priority                VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL
                            CHECK (priority IN (
                                'Low','Medium','High','Critical'
                            )),
    -- Times in minutes
    first_response_minutes  INT NOT NULL,
    resolution_minutes      INT NOT NULL,
    -- Business hours only?
    business_hours_only     BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    -- Escalation
    warning_at_pct          SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 75,
    escalate_to_role        VARCHAR(100),
    is_active               BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at              TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by              UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_sla_category_priority 
    ON crm.sla_rules(category_id, priority);

-- ============================================================
-- TICKETS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.tickets (
    id                      UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    ticket_number           VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    subject                 VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    description             TEXT,
    -- Source
    source                  VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Manual'
                            CHECK (source IN (
                                'Email','Phone','WhatsApp',
                                'WalkIn','Portal','System'
                            )),
    -- Customer link
    contact_id              UUID REFERENCES crm.contacts(id),
    account_id              UUID REFERENCES crm.accounts(id),
    customer_id             UUID,
    -- Classification
    category_id             UUID REFERENCES crm.ticket_categories(id),
    priority                VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Medium'
                            CHECK (priority IN (
                                'Low','Medium','High','Critical'
                            )),
    -- Reference to order/invoice if complaint is product-related
    reference_type          VARCHAR(50),
    reference_id            UUID,
    -- Status
    status                  VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Open'
                            CHECK (status IN (
                                'Open','InProgress','WaitingOnCustomer',
                                'WaitingOnThirdParty','Resolved',
                                'Closed','Cancelled'
                            )),
    -- Assignment
    branch_id               UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    assigned_to             UUID,
    assigned_at             TIMESTAMPTZ,
    -- SLA
    sla_rule_id             UUID REFERENCES crm.sla_rules(id),
    first_response_due_at   TIMESTAMPTZ,
    first_responded_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    resolution_due_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    resolved_at             TIMESTAMPTZ,
    closed_at               TIMESTAMPTZ,
    sla_status              VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'OnTrack'
                            CHECK (sla_status IN (
                                'OnTrack','Warning','Breached','Paused'
                            )),
    sla_breach_notified     BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    sla_paused_at           TIMESTAMPTZ,
    sla_paused_duration_min INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    -- Resolution
    resolution_notes        TEXT,
    root_cause              TEXT,
    -- CSAT
    csat_score              SMALLINT CHECK (csat_score BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    csat_comment            TEXT,
    csat_collected_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    -- Meta
    tags                    TEXT[],
    is_repeat               BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    repeat_of_ticket_id     UUID REFERENCES crm.tickets(id),
    created_at              TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by              UUID,
    updated_at              TIMESTAMPTZ,
    updated_by              UUID
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_tickets_number ON crm.tickets(ticket_number);
CREATE INDEX ix_tickets_status ON crm.tickets(status) 
    WHERE status NOT IN ('Resolved','Closed','Cancelled');
CREATE INDEX ix_tickets_assigned ON crm.tickets(assigned_to);
CREATE INDEX ix_tickets_contact ON crm.tickets(contact_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_tickets_sla_due 
    ON crm.tickets(resolution_due_at, sla_status) 
    WHERE sla_status IN ('OnTrack','Warning');
CREATE INDEX ix_tickets_branch ON crm.tickets(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_tickets_created ON crm.tickets(created_at DESC);

-- ============================================================
-- TICKET COMMENTS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.ticket_comments (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    ticket_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.tickets(id),
    comment_type    VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Public'
                    CHECK (comment_type IN ('Public','Internal','System')),
    body            TEXT NOT NULL,
    author_id       UUID NOT NULL,
    author_type     VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'User'
                    CHECK (author_type IN ('User','Customer','System')),
    is_solution     BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ
);

CREATE INDEX ix_tc_ticket ON crm.ticket_comments(ticket_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_tc_created ON crm.ticket_comments(created_at);

-- ============================================================
-- SLA TIMERS (audit trail of all SLA events)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.sla_timers (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    ticket_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.tickets(id),
    event_type      VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL
                    CHECK (event_type IN (
                        'Started','Paused','Resumed','Warning',
                        'Breached','Resolved','Escalated'
                    )),
    occurred_at     TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    time_elapsed_min INT,
    notes           TEXT
);

CREATE INDEX ix_sla_timers_ticket ON crm.sla_timers(ticket_id);

-- ============================================================
-- TICKET ESCALATIONS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.ticket_escalations (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    ticket_id           UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.tickets(id),
    escalated_from_user UUID,
    escalated_to_user   UUID,
    escalated_to_role   VARCHAR(100),
    reason              TEXT NOT NULL,
    escalated_at        TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    resolved_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    resolution_notes    TEXT
);

CREATE INDEX ix_te_ticket ON crm.ticket_escalations(ticket_id);

-- ============================================================
-- KNOWLEDGE BASE ARTICLES
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.kb_articles (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    title           VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    slug            VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    content         TEXT NOT NULL,
    summary         TEXT,
    category_id     UUID REFERENCES crm.ticket_categories(id),
    status          VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Draft'
                    CHECK (status IN ('Draft','Published','Archived')),
    views_count     INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    helpful_count   INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    not_helpful_count INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    tags            TEXT[],
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ,
    published_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_at      TIMESTAMPTZ
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uix_kb_slug ON crm.kb_articles(slug) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_kb_category ON crm.kb_articles(category_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_kb_status ON crm.kb_articles(status);
CREATE INDEX ix_kb_search 
    ON crm.kb_articles 
    USING gin(to_tsvector('english', title || ' ' || COALESCE(summary, '')));

-- ============================================================
-- SURVEYS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.surveys (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    title           VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    description     TEXT,
    survey_type     VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'General'
                    CHECK (survey_type IN (
                        'CSAT','NPS','General','PostPurchase',
                        'ProductFeedback'
                    )),
    status          VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Draft'
                    CHECK (status IN ('Draft','Active','Closed')),
    starts_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    ends_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    target_segment_id UUID REFERENCES crm.segments(id),
    response_count  INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    nps_score       NUMERIC(5,2),                -- calculated
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by      UUID,
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ
);

-- ============================================================
-- SURVEY QUESTIONS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.survey_questions (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    survey_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.surveys(id),
    question_order  SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    question_text   VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    question_type   VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL
                    CHECK (question_type IN (
                        'NPS','Rating','MultipleChoice',
                        'Text','YesNo','Scale'
                    )),
    options         JSONB,           -- for MultipleChoice: [{label, value}]
    min_label       VARCHAR(50),     -- for Scale: "Not likely"
    max_label       VARCHAR(50),     -- for Scale: "Extremely likely"
    is_required     BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    CONSTRAINT uix_sq_order UNIQUE(survey_id, question_order)
);

CREATE INDEX ix_sq_survey ON crm.survey_questions(survey_id);

-- ============================================================
-- SURVEY RESPONSES
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.survey_responses (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    survey_id       UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.surveys(id),
    contact_id      UUID REFERENCES crm.contacts(id),
    customer_id     UUID,
    -- NPS Category
    nps_category    VARCHAR(20)
                    CHECK (nps_category IN (
                        'Promoter','Passive','Detractor'
                    )),
    submitted_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    ip_address      INET,
    is_complete     BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE
);

CREATE INDEX ix_sr_survey ON crm.survey_responses(survey_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_sr_contact ON crm.survey_responses(contact_id);

-- ============================================================
-- SURVEY ANSWERS
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE crm.survey_answers (
    id                  UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    survey_response_id  UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.survey_responses(id),
    question_id         UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crm.survey_questions(id),
    answer_text         TEXT,
    answer_number       NUMERIC(5,2),
    answer_option       VARCHAR(200),
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_sa_response ON crm.survey_answers(survey_response_id);
```

# PHASE 3 — CRM API Design

## 3.1 CRM Endpoints

http

```
# ── CONTACTS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
GET    /api/v1/crm/contacts
POST   /api/v1/crm/contacts
GET    /api/v1/crm/contacts/{id}
PUT    /api/v1/crm/contacts/{id}
DELETE /api/v1/crm/contacts/{id}
GET    /api/v1/crm/contacts/{id}/360-view        # Full timeline
GET    /api/v1/crm/contacts/{id}/activities
GET    /api/v1/crm/contacts/{id}/tickets
GET    /api/v1/crm/contacts/{id}/loyalty
POST   /api/v1/crm/contacts/merge                # Merge duplicates
POST   /api/v1/crm/contacts/import               # Bulk CSV import
GET    /api/v1/crm/contacts/export               # Export CSV

# ── ACCOUNTS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
GET    /api/v1/crm/accounts
POST   /api/v1/crm/accounts
GET    /api/v1/crm/accounts/{id}
PUT    /api/v1/crm/accounts/{id}
DELETE /api/v1/crm/accounts/{id}
GET    /api/v1/crm/accounts/{id}/contacts
GET    /api/v1/crm/accounts/{id}/opportunities

# ── LEADS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
GET    /api/v1/crm/leads
POST   /api/v1/crm/leads
GET    /api/v1/crm/leads/{id}
PUT    /api/v1/crm/leads/{id}
POST   /api/v1/crm/leads/{id}/qualify
POST   /api/v1/crm/leads/{id}/disqualify
POST   /api/v1/crm/leads/{id}/convert
POST   /api/v1/crm/leads/{id}/assign
GET    /api/v1/crm/leads/{id}/activities
POST   /api/v1/crm/leads/import

# ── OPPORTUNITIES ─────────────────────────────────────────────
GET    /api/v1/crm/opportunities
POST   /api/v1/crm/opportunities
GET    /api/v1/crm/opportunities/{id}
PUT    /api/v1/crm/opportunities/{id}
POST   /api/v1/crm/opportunities/{id}/move-stage
POST   /api/v1/crm/opportunities/{id}/close-won
POST   /api/v1/crm/opportunities/{id}/close-lost
GET    /api/v1/crm/opportunities/pipeline          # Kanban view
GET    /api/v1/crm/opportunities/forecast          # Forecast report

# ── ACTIVITIES ────────────────────────────────────────────────
GET    /api/v1/crm/activities
POST   /api/v1/crm/activities
GET    /api/v1/crm/activities/{id}
PUT    /api/v1/crm/activities/{id}
POST   /api/v1/crm/activities/{id}/complete
POST   /api/v1/crm/activities/{id}/reschedule
GET    /api/v1/crm/activities/today               # Today's activities
GET    /api/v1/crm/activities/overdue             # Overdue tasks

# ── CAMPAIGNS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
GET    /api/v1/crm/campaigns
POST   /api/v1/crm/campaigns
GET    /api/v1/crm/campaigns/{id}
PUT    /api/v1/crm/campaigns/{id}
POST   /api/v1/crm/campaigns/{id}/schedule
POST   /api/v1/crm/campaigns/{id}/send-now
POST   /api/v1/crm/campaigns/{id}/pause
POST   /api/v1/crm/campaigns/{id}/cancel
GET    /api/v1/crm/campaigns/{id}/metrics
GET    /api/v1/crm/campaigns/{id}/recipients

# ── DRIP CAMPAIGNS ────────────────────────────────────────────
GET    /api/v1/crm/drip-campaigns
POST   /api/v1/crm/drip-campaigns
GET    /api/v1/crm/drip-campaigns/{id}
PUT    /api/v1/crm/drip-campaigns/{id}
POST   /api/v1/crm/drip-campaigns/{id}/enroll     # Manually enroll contact
GET    /api/v1/crm/drip-campaigns/{id}/enrollments

# ── LOYALTY ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
GET    /api/v1/crm/loyalty/accounts
GET    /api/v1/crm/loyalty/accounts/{id}
GET    /api/v1/crm/loyalty/accounts/{id}/transactions
POST   /api/v1/crm/loyalty/accounts/{id}/redeem
POST   /api/v1/crm/loyalty/accounts/{id}/adjust   # Manual adjustment
GET    /api/v1/crm/loyalty/accounts/{id}/statement # PDF statement
GET    /api/v1/crm/loyalty/tiers
POST   /api/v1/crm/loyalty/tiers
PUT    /api/v1/crm/loyalty/tiers/{id}
GET    /api/v1/crm/loyalty/rules
POST   /api/v1/crm/loyalty/rules
GET    /api/v1/crm/loyalty/dashboard

# ── SEGMENTS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
GET    /api/v1/crm/segments
POST   /api/v1/crm/segments
GET    /api/v1/crm/segments/{id}
PUT    /api/v1/crm/segments/{id}
POST   /api/v1/crm/segments/{id}/refresh          # Manually refresh
GET    /api/v1/crm/segments/{id}/members
GET    /api/v1/crm/segments/rfm                   # RFM segment view

# ── TICKETS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
GET    /api/v1/crm/tickets
POST   /api/v1/crm/tickets
GET    /api/v1/crm/tickets/{id}
PUT    /api/v1/crm/tickets/{id}
POST   /api/v1/crm/tickets/{id}/comments
GET    /api/v1/crm/tickets/{id}/comments
POST   /api/v1/crm/tickets/{id}/assign
POST   /api/v1/crm/tickets/{id}/resolve
POST   /api/v1/crm/tickets/{id}/close
POST   /api/v1/crm/tickets/{id}/escalate
POST   /api/v1/crm/tickets/{id}/csat              # Submit CSAT rating
GET    /api/v1/crm/tickets/sla-dashboard
GET    /api/v1/crm/tickets/my-open               # Agent's open tickets

# ── KNOWLEDGE BASE ────────────────────────────────────────────
GET    /api/v1/crm/kb/articles
POST   /api/v1/crm/kb/articles
GET    /api/v1/crm/kb/articles/{id}
PUT    /api/v1/crm/kb/articles/{id}
POST   /api/v1/crm/kb/articles/{id}/publish
GET    /api/v1/crm/kb/search?q=return+policy

# ── SURVEYS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
GET    /api/v1/crm/surveys
POST   /api/v1/crm/surveys
GET    /api/v1/crm/surveys/{id}
PUT    /api/v1/crm/surveys/{id}
POST   /api/v1/crm/surveys/{id}/activate
GET    /api/v1/crm/surveys/{id}/responses
GET    /api/v1/crm/surveys/{id}/analytics

# ── REPORTS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
GET    /api/v1/crm/reports/pipeline-summary
GET    /api/v1/crm/reports/lead-funnel
GET    /api/v1/crm/reports/campaign-roi
GET    /api/v1/crm/reports/ticket-sla-compliance
GET    /api/v1/crm/reports/loyalty-summary
GET    /api/v1/crm/reports/rfm-distribution
GET    /api/v1/crm/reports/csat-trend
GET    /api/v1/crm/reports/nps-trend
GET    /api/v1/crm/reports/sales-rep-performance
```

## 3.2 Key CRM DTOs

csharp

```
// ─── LEAD DTOs ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

public record CreateLeadRequest(
    string FirstName,
    string? LastName,
    string? CompanyName,
    string? Email,
    string? Phone,
    string? InterestProduct,
    decimal? EstimatedValue,
    Guid? SourceId,
    string? UtmSource,
    string? UtmCampaign,
    string? Notes,
    List<string>? Tags
);

public record ConvertLeadRequest(
    bool CreateContact,
    bool CreateAccount,
    bool CreateOpportunity,
    string? OpportunityName,
    decimal? OpportunityValue,
    DateOnly? ExpectedCloseDate,
    Guid? StageId
);

public record LeadListResponse(
    Guid Id,
    string LeadNumber,
    string FullName,
    string? CompanyName,
    string? Email,
    string? Phone,
    string Status,
    string ScoreGrade,
    int Score,
    string? SourceName,
    string? AssignedToName,
    DateOnly? ExpectedCloseDate,
    decimal? EstimatedValue,
    DateTime CreatedAt,
    DateTime? LastActivityAt
);

// ─── OPPORTUNITY DTOs ───────────────────────────────────────────

public record CreateOpportunityRequest(
    string Name,
    Guid? ContactId,
    Guid? AccountId,
    Guid StageId,
    decimal EstimatedValue,
    DateOnly ExpectedCloseDate,
    string? Description,
    List<OpportunityProductRequest>? Products
);

public record MoveStageRequest(
    Guid NewStageId,
    decimal? UpdatedProbability,
    string? Notes
);

public record CloseWonRequest(
    decimal ActualValue,
    bool CreateSalesOrder,
    string? Notes
);

public record CloseLostRequest(
    string Reason,
    string? LostToCompetitor,
    string? Notes
);

public record OpportunityPipelineResponse(
    List<PipelineStageGroup> Stages
);

public record PipelineStageGroup(
    Guid StageId,
    string StageName,
    decimal WinProbability,
    int OpportunityCount,
    decimal TotalValue,
    decimal WeightedValue,
    List<OpportunityCardResponse> Opportunities
);

// ─── TICKET DTOs ────────────────────────────────────────────────

public record CreateTicketRequest(
    string Subject,
    string? Description,
    string Source,
    Guid? ContactId,
    Guid? AccountId,
    Guid? CategoryId,
    string Priority,
    string? ReferenceType,
    Guid? ReferenceId,
    List<string>? Tags
);

public record TicketResponse(
    Guid Id,
    string TicketNumber,
    string Subject,
    string Status,
    string Priority,
    string SlaStatus,
    string? CategoryName,
    string? ContactName,
    string? AssignedToName,
    DateTime? FirstResponseDueAt,
    DateTime? ResolutionDueAt,
    int? TimeRemainingMinutes,
    DateTime CreatedAt,
    List<TicketCommentResponse> RecentComments
);

// ─── LOYALTY DTOs ───────────────────────────────────────────────

public record LoyaltyAccountResponse(
    Guid Id,
    string AccountNumber,
    string ContactName,
    string TierName,
    string TierColor,
    decimal PointsBalance,
    decimal LifetimeEarned,
    decimal LifetimeRedeemed,
    string? ReferralCode,
    DateTime? TierReviewDate,
    List<LoyaltyTransactionResponse> RecentTransactions
);

public record RedeemPointsRequest(
    decimal Points,
    string? ReferenceType,
    Guid? ReferenceId
);

// ─── CAMPAIGN DTOs ──────────────────────────────────────────────

public record CampaignMetricsResponse(
    int TotalSent,
    int Delivered,
    int Opened,
    int Clicked,
    int Converted,
    int Bounced,
    int Unsubscribed,
    decimal DeliveryRate,
    decimal OpenRate,
    decimal ClickRate,
    decimal ConversionRate,
    decimal Roi,
    decimal RevenueGenerated
);
```

# PHASE 4 — CRM Architecture (Modular Clean Architecture)

---

## 4.1 CRM Module Structure

text

```
src/Modules/CRM/
├── CMR.Modules.CRM.Domain/
│   ├── Aggregates/
│   │   ├── Lead/
│   │   │   ├── Lead.cs
│   │   │   ├── LeadScore.cs
│   │   │   └── LeadStatus.cs
│   │   ├── Contact/
│   │   │   ├── Contact.cs
│   │   │   └── CommunicationPreference.cs
│   │   ├── Account/
│   │   │   └── Account.cs
│   │   ├── Opportunity/
│   │   │   ├── Opportunity.cs
│   │   │   ├── OpportunityStage.cs
│   │   │   └── OpportunityStatus.cs
│   │   ├── Ticket/
│   │   │   ├── Ticket.cs
│   │   │   ├── SlaRule.cs
│   │   │   └── TicketStatus.cs
│   │   ├── Loyalty/
│   │   │   ├── LoyaltyAccount.cs
│   │   │   ├── LoyaltyTransaction.cs
│   │   │   └── LoyaltyTier.cs
│   │   └── Campaign/
│   │       ├── Campaign.cs
│   │       └── DripCampaign.cs
│   │
│   ├── DomainEvents/
│   │   ├── LeadCreatedEvent.cs
│   │   ├── LeadConvertedEvent.cs
│   │   ├── LeadScoredHighEvent.cs
│   │   ├── OpportunityWonEvent.cs
│   │   ├── OpportunityLostEvent.cs
│   │   ├── TicketCreatedEvent.cs
```

```
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TICKET AGGREGATE
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public sealed class Ticket : AggregateRoot<Guid>
{
    private readonly List<TicketComment> _comments = new();

    public string TicketNumber { get; private set; }
    public string Subject { get; private set; }
    public TicketStatus Status { get; private set; }
    public TicketPriority Priority { get; private set; }
    public SlaStatus SlaStatus { get; private set; }
    public Guid? ContactId { get; private set; }
    public Guid? AssignedTo { get; private set; }
    public DateTime? FirstResponseDueAt { get; private set; }
    public DateTime? ResolutionDueAt { get; private set; }
    public DateTime? FirstRespondedAt { get; private set; }
    public DateTime? ResolvedAt { get; private set; }
    public IReadOnlyList<TicketComment> Comments => _comments.AsReadOnly();

    private Ticket() { }

    public static Ticket Create(
        string ticketNumber,
        string subject,
        string description,
        Guid? contactId,
        TicketPriority priority,
        SlaRule slaRule,
        Guid branchId)
    {
        var now = DateTime.UtcNow;
        var ticket = new Ticket
        {
            Id = Guid.NewGuid(),
            TicketNumber = ticketNumber,
            Subject = subject,
            Status = TicketStatus.Open,
            Priority = priority,
            SlaStatus = SlaStatus.OnTrack,
            ContactId = contactId,
            FirstResponseDueAt = now.AddMinutes(slaRule.FirstResponseMinutes),
            ResolutionDueAt = now.AddMinutes(slaRule.ResolutionMinutes)
        };

        ticket.RaiseDomainEvent(new TicketCreatedEvent(
            ticket.Id, priority, slaRule.Id));
        return ticket;
    }

    public void AddComment(string body, Guid authorId, bool isPublic)
    {
        if (Status == TicketStatus.Closed)
            throw new DomainException("Cannot comment on a closed ticket.");

        var comment = TicketComment.Create(Id, body, authorId,
            isPublic ? CommentType.Public : CommentType.Internal);
        _comments.Add(comment);

        // Record first response
        if (FirstRespondedAt is null && isPublic)
        {
            FirstRespondedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
        }
    }

    public void Resolve(string resolutionNotes, Guid resolvedBy)
    {
        if (Status == TicketStatus.Resolved || Status == TicketStatus.Closed)
            throw new DomainException("Ticket already resolved or closed.");

        Status = TicketStatus.Resolved;
        ResolvedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

        RaiseDomainEvent(new TicketResolvedEvent(
            Id, ContactId, ResolvedAt.Value, Priority));
    }

    public void MarkSlaBreached()
    {
        SlaStatus = SlaStatus.Breached;
        RaiseDomainEvent(new TicketSlaBreachedEvent(Id, Priority, AssignedTo));
    }

    public void Assign(Guid userId)
    {
        AssignedTo = userId;
        Status = TicketStatus.InProgress;
    }

    public void PauseSla(DateTime pausedAt)
    {
        SlaStatus = SlaStatus.Paused;
        // Domain event to track pause
        RaiseDomainEvent(new TicketSlaPausedEvent(Id, pausedAt));
    }
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// LOYALTY ACCOUNT AGGREGATE
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public sealed class LoyaltyAccount : AggregateRoot<Guid>
{
    public string AccountNumber { get; private set; }
    public Guid? CustomerId { get; private set; }
    public Guid TierId { get; private set; }
    public decimal PointsBalance { get; private set; }
    public decimal LifetimeEarned { get; private set; }
    public decimal LifetimeRedeemed { get; private set; }
    public string? ReferralCode { get; private set; }

    private LoyaltyAccount() { }

    public static LoyaltyAccount Create(
        string accountNumber,
        Guid? customerId,
        Guid bronzeTierId,
        string? referralCode = null)
    {
        var account = new LoyaltyAccount
        {
            Id = Guid.NewGuid(),
            AccountNumber = accountNumber,
            CustomerId = customerId,
            TierId = bronzeTierId,
            PointsBalance = 0,
            LifetimeEarned = 0,
            LifetimeRedeemed = 0,
            ReferralCode = referralCode ?? GenerateReferralCode()
        };

        account.RaiseDomainEvent(
            new LoyaltyAccountCreatedEvent(account.Id, customerId));
        return account;
    }

    public LoyaltyTransaction EarnPoints(
        decimal points,
        string description,
        string? referenceType,
        Guid? referenceId,
        Guid? ruleId,
        DateTime? expiresAt)
    {
        if (points <= 0)
            throw new DomainException("Points must be positive.");

        PointsBalance += points;
        LifetimeEarned += points;

        var transaction = LoyaltyTransaction.Create(
            Id, TransactionType.Earn, points,
            PointsBalance, description,
            referenceType, referenceId, ruleId, expiresAt);

        RaiseDomainEvent(new LoyaltyPointsEarnedEvent(
            Id, CustomerId, points, PointsBalance));
        return transaction;
    }

    public LoyaltyTransaction RedeemPoints(
        decimal points,
        string description,
        string? referenceType,
        Guid? referenceId)
    {
        if (points <= 0)
            throw new DomainException("Points must be positive.");

        if (points > PointsBalance)
            throw new DomainException(
                $"Insufficient points. Available: {PointsBalance}");

        PointsBalance -= points;
        LifetimeRedeemed += points;

        var transaction = LoyaltyTransaction.Create(
            Id, TransactionType.Redeem, -points,
            PointsBalance, description,
            referenceType, referenceId, null, null);

        RaiseDomainEvent(new LoyaltyPointsRedeemedEvent(
            Id, CustomerId, points, PointsBalance));
        return transaction;
    }

    public bool TryUpgradeTier(LoyaltyTier newTier)
    {
        if (newTier.Id == TierId) return false;
        if (PointsBalance < newTier.MinPoints) return false;

        var oldTierId = TierId;
        TierId = newTier.Id;

        RaiseDomainEvent(new LoyaltyTierUpgradedEvent(
            Id, CustomerId, oldTierId, newTier.Id, newTier.Name));
        return true;
    }

    private static string GenerateReferralCode()
        => "REF" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N")[..7].ToUpper();
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// DAPPER QUERY: PIPELINE REPORT
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public class GetPipelineHandler
    : IQueryHandler<GetPipelineQuery, OpportunityPipelineResponse>
{
    private readonly IDbConnectionFactory _db;
    private readonly ICurrentUserService _user;

    public async Task<OpportunityPipelineResponse> Handle(
        GetPipelineQuery query,
        CancellationToken ct)
    {
        const string sql = """
            SELECT
                os.id               AS stage_id,
                os.name             AS stage_name,
                os.win_probability,
                os.stage_order,
                COUNT(o.id)         AS opportunity_count,
                COALESCE(SUM(o.estimated_value), 0) AS total_value,
                COALESCE(SUM(o.weighted_value), 0)  AS weighted_value,
                -- opportunity details as JSON
                JSON_AGG(
                    JSON_BUILD_OBJECT(
                        'id',               o.id,
                        'number',           o.opportunity_number,
                        'name',             o.name,
                        'estimatedValue',   o.estimated_value,
                        'weightedValue',    o.weighted_value,
                        'expectedCloseDate',o.expected_close_date,
                        'contactName',      c.full_name,
                        'accountName',      a.name,
                        'assignedTo',       up.full_name,
                        'isStale',          o.is_stale,
                        'daysSinceActivity',
                            EXTRACT(DAY FROM NOW() - o.last_activity_at)
                    ) ORDER BY o.estimated_value DESC
                ) FILTER (WHERE o.id IS NOT NULL) AS opportunities
            FROM crm.opportunity_stages os
            LEFT JOIN crm.opportunities o 
                ON o.stage_id = os.id
                AND o.status = 'Open'
                AND o.deleted_at IS NULL
                AND o.branch_id = @BranchId
                AND (@AssignedTo IS NULL OR o.assigned_to = @AssignedTo)
            LEFT JOIN crm.contacts c ON c.id = o.contact_id
            LEFT JOIN crm.accounts a ON a.id = o.account_id
            LEFT JOIN user_profiles up ON up.user_id = o.assigned_to
            WHERE os.is_active = TRUE
            GROUP BY os.id, os.name, os.win_probability, os.stage_order
            ORDER BY os.stage_order;
        """;

        using var connection = _db.CreateConnection();
        var stages = await connection.QueryAsync<PipelineStageRow>(
            sql,
            new
            {
                BranchId = _user.BranchId,
                AssignedTo = query.AssignedTo
            });

        var stageGroups = stages.Select(s => new PipelineStageGroup(
            s.StageId,
            s.StageName,
            s.WinProbability,
            s.OpportunityCount,
            s.TotalValue,
            s.WeightedValue,
            DeserializeOpportunities(s.Opportunities)
        )).ToList();

        return new OpportunityPipelineResponse(stageGroups);
    }
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// INTEGRATION EVENT HANDLER: Award loyalty on payment
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
public class PaymentReceivedIntegrationHandler
    : IIntegrationEventHandler<PaymentReceivedIntegrationEvent>
{
    private readonly ILoyaltyAccountRepository _loyaltyRepo;
    private readonly ILoyaltyRulesEngine _rulesEngine;
    private readonly IUnitOfWork _unitOfWork;
    private readonly INotificationService _notifications;

    public async Task Handle(
        PaymentReceivedIntegrationEvent evt,
        CancellationToken ct)
    {
        // Find loyalty account for this customer
        var loyaltyAccount = await _loyaltyRepo
            .GetByCustomerIdAsync(evt.CustomerId, ct);

        if (loyaltyAccount is null)
        {
            // Create new loyalty account
            loyaltyAccount = await CreateLoyaltyAccountAsync(
                evt.CustomerId, ct);
        }

        // Calculate points using rules engine
        var pointsResult = await _rulesEngine.CalculateEarnPointsAsync(
            loyaltyAccount,
            evt.Amount,
            evt.InvoiceId,
            ct);

        if (pointsResult.Points <= 0) return;

        // Earn points on aggregate
        var transaction = loyaltyAccount.EarnPoints(
            pointsResult.Points,
            $"Purchase on Invoice #{evt.InvoiceNumber}",
            "SalesInvoice",
            evt.InvoiceId,
            pointsResult.RuleId,
            DateTime.UtcNow.AddYears(1)  // expires in 1 year
        );

        // Check tier upgrade
        var nextTier = await _loyaltyRepo
            .GetNextTierAsync(loyaltyAccount.TierId, ct);
        if (nextTier is not null)
            loyaltyAccount.TryUpgradeTier(nextTier);

        // Persist
        _loyaltyRepo.Update(loyaltyAccount);
        await _unitOfWork.SaveChangesAsync(ct);

        // Send notification to customer
        await _notifications.SendAsync(new LoyaltyEarnNotification(
            evt.CustomerId,
            pointsResult.Points,
            loyaltyAccount.PointsBalance));
    }
}
```

# CMR ERP System — Complete End-to-End Blueprint

## Explicit Assumptions (proceeding with defaults)

text

```
✅ CMR = Commerce & Retail Management
✅ Industry = Retail / Distribution (multi-branch)
✅ Branches = 5 locations, 1 country (India)
✅ Concurrent Users = ~300 users
✅ Daily Transactions = ~8,000 (orders, invoices, stock movements)
✅ Online-only (stable internet at all branches)
✅ Compliance = GST, e-invoicing (IRN/QR), TDS, Labor laws
✅ Integrations = Payment gateway, SMS, Email, Bank reconciliation
✅ Infrastructure = Cloud (AWS/Azure), PostgreSQL RDS
✅ Uptime SLA = 99.5%
✅ Multi-tenant = No (single org, multi-branch)
✅ Security = PII heavy (customer data, employee data, bank details)
```
